### [36] [Learning Features, Not Layouts: Feature-Axis Attention as a Transfer Prior for Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13012)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将全局注意力的轴从空间转向特征（通道），可显著提升自然图像预训练模型向医学影像的迁移能力，而从头训练性能基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像预训练的基础模型迁移到医学影像时常因域偏移、模态异质性和标签稀缺而不可靠。作者将部分原因归咎于一个被忽视的设计选择：标准ViT把所有全局建模放在空间token的自注意力上，这符合自然场景的目标布局先验，但医学影像的判别信号在于跨扫描仪重现的特征相关性与纹理，而非布局。因此作者追问：全局注意力的轴（空间 vs 特征）本身是否是可迁移医学表征的一个杠杆。

Method: 设计FAD-ViT骨干网络，严格解耦空间轴与特征轴，以特征轴注意力作为主要的全局算子，并把注意力轴作为可控变量。在匹配tokenization、容量和实验协议的前提下，跨23个分类、分割和低数据任务进行全面评测。

Result: 把全局注意力从空间轴转向特征轴后，从头训练性能基本不变，但迁移能力明显增强：相同的ImageNet预训练带来最高约三倍的迁移增益，在每个留出分割任务上均有稳定提升，参数量最多仅为标准与通道注意力基线的四分之一，计算量也低得多。优势在纹理（而非几何）更具判别性的场景中最大，与预训练特征的更多复用相吻合。

Conclusion: 全局注意力的轴是医学迁移的一个设计杠杆，以通道（特征）为主是一个简单、廉价且有原则的选择。

Abstract: Natural-image-pretrained foundation models are increasingly adapted to medical imaging, yet transfer remains unreliable under domain shift, modality heterogeneity, and label scarcity. We trace part of this fragility to an overlooked design choice. Standard vision transformers place all global modeling on self-attention over spatial tokens, an object-layout prior well suited to natural scenes but not to medical images, where the discriminative signal lies in feature correlations and texture that recur across scanners while layout does not. We ask whether the axis of global attention, spatial versus feature, is itself a lever for transferable medical representations. Existing channel-aware hybrids confound this question, adding channel attention to a spatially-mixed backbone and leaving the axes coupled and the feature axis unmeasurable in isolation. We design FAD-ViT, a backbone that strictly decouples the two axes, making feature-axis attention the primary global operator and the attention axis a controlled variable. Evaluating comprehensively across 23 classification, segmentation, and low-data tasks under matched tokenization, capacity, and protocol, we find that shifting global attention from the spatial to the feature axis leaves from-scratch performance essentially unchanged, yet markedly strengthens transfer. Identical ImageNet pretraining yields up to roughly three times the transfer gain, with consistent improvements on every held-out segmentation task at up to a quarter the parameter count and far lower compute than standard and channel-attention baselines. The advantage is largest where texture rather than geometry is discriminative, tracking greater reuse of pretrained features. The axis of global attention is thus a design lever for medical transfer, and making it channel-primary is a simple, cheap, and principled choice.

</details>


### [37] [Unified Generative Video Compression with Autoregressive and Bidirectional Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13023)
*Tianyu Zhang,Shuai Huo,Dengchao Jin,Hewei Liu,Meng Lei,Tianwen Wu,Jiawen Gu,Chao Zhou,Li Li,Dong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGVC 是统一生成式视频编解码器，同一码流支持 BI 与 AR 两种解码模式，兼顾高质量重放与低延迟直播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在超低码率下重建质量好，但存在部署权衡：双向模型质量高却需完整序列、延迟大；自回归模型可流式低延迟，却因时序上下文受限导致压缩效率下降。尤其当视频既要实时解码又要存储供后续高质量回放时，单一模式难以兼顾。

Method: 提出 UniGVC，从单一码流支持两种解码模式。UniGVC-BI 采用双向 DiT 与密集注意力，用于面向质量随机访问解码；UniGVC-AR 采用窗口化 AR-DiT 用于低延迟流式。两条路径共享分层潜码编解码器，递推产生相同条件且码率极低，使 BI 可作为条件教师对 AR 进行单步蒸馏；另用小型条件视频解码器进一步降低流式延迟。

Result: 在标准 1080p 基准上，UniGVC-BI 在所评估方法中取得最佳整体率-失真-感知权衡，低至 0.0005 bpp 仍生成时序稳定视频；UniGVC-AR 保持竞争力，1080p 解码达 36.85 FPS，比 BI 快 250 倍以上。

Conclusion: UniGVC 通过单一码流统一 BI 与 AR 解码模式，同时满足高质量重放与低延迟直播需求。代码与模型将后续发布。

Abstract: Video diffusion models can reconstruct visually compelling content from severely compressed representations at ultra-low bitrates, but they impose a rigid deployment trade-off. Bidirectional (BI) models achieve high quality through dense full-sequence attention, yet require the entire sequence before reconstruction and incur substantial latency. Autoregressive (AR) models enable low-delay streaming, but their restricted temporal context reduces compression efficiency. This tension is particularly relevant when a video stream must be decoded in real time yet is also stored for later, quality-oriented playback, making AR and BI models natural choices for the live and replay stages, respectively. To support both stages, we present UniGVC, a Unified Generative Video Codec that supports two decoding modes from a single bitstream. UniGVC-BI employs a BI-DiT with dense attention for quality-oriented random-access decoding, whereas UniGVC-AR uses windowed AR-DiT for low-delay streaming. Both paths share a hierarchical latent codec that recurrently produces the same conditions with ultra-low bitrates, allowing UniGVC-BI to serve as a conditional teacher for single-step distillation of UniGVC-AR. A tiny conditional video decoder further reduces streaming latency while preserving most of the reconstruction quality. On standard 1080p benchmarks, UniGVC-BI achieves the best overall rate-distortion-perception trade-off among the evaluated methods and produces temporally stable video at rates as low as 0.0005 bits per pixel. Meanwhile, UniGVC-AR remains competitive while decoding 1080p video at 36.85 FPS, over $250\times$ faster than UniGVC-BI. Code and models will be released later.

</details>


### [38] [What Visual Representations Do World Action Models Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13024)
*Kai Liu,Jiangran Lyu,Tingyu Deng,Yixin Zheng,Jingtian Wan,Wenbo Cui,Mi Yan,Yizhou Zhou,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了世界动作模型（WAMs）中的视觉表示与集成机制，提出表示配方R²-WAM，在RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop上达到84.7%、RoboTwin 2.0上达到92.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）继承了预训练视频生成模型的强大先验，但其VAE表示是为视觉重建优化的，而非操作任务。外部视觉表示可改善操作性能，但现有方法将表示选择与不同集成机制耦合在一起，难以区分各自的独立效果。

Method: 在受控设置下系统比较视觉表示、五种集成机制及特征组合。基于研究结论形成"表示配方"（Representation Recipe），实例化为R²-WAM模型。

Result: 三大发现：一是强通用视觉表示带来更广的鲁棒性提升，可匹配或超越针对特定视觉能力预训练的表示；二是通道级联合预测取得最高平均成功率，未来特征预测与动作专家对新增视觉信息的有效利用是其增益来源；三是额外来源应依据其超出已有表示的任务相关信息进行选择。R²-WAM在RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop达84.7%，RoboTwin 2.0达92.8%，较仅VAE基线分别提升5.9和2.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 即使是评估中最优的组合，其额外增益也小于最初将强通用表示加入仅VAE基线所带来的提升。未来工作应优先发展更强的通用视觉表示及更有效的集成机制，特征组合仅作为补充改进途径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) inherit powerful priors from pretrained video generation models, but their variational autoencoder (VAE) representations are optimized for visual reconstruction rather than manipulation. External visual representations can improve manipulation, but existing methods couple representation choices with different integration mechanisms, obscuring their individual effects. We systematically compare visual representations, five integration mechanisms, and feature combinations under controlled settings. Our study yields three main findings. First, strong general-purpose visual representations provide broader robustness gains and can match or outperform representations pretrained for specific visual capabilities. Second, channel-wise joint prediction achieves the highest average success rate; further analyses suggest that future-feature prediction and the action expert's effective use of added visual information contribute to its gains. Third, additional sources should be selected for the task-relevant information they contribute beyond existing representations, as the same source can help one combination but harm another. However, even the best evaluated composition provides a smaller additional gain than the initial improvement from adding a strong general-purpose representation to the VAE-only baseline. We instantiate the resulting Representation Recipe as R$^2$-WAM. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop with an 84.7% success rate and reaches 92.8% on RoboTwin 2.0, improving over the VAE-only baselines by 5.9 and 2.2 percentage points, respectively. Taken together, these findings suggest that future work should prioritize stronger general-purpose visual representations and more effective mechanisms for integrating them into WAMs, with feature composition serving as a supplementary route to improvement.

</details>


### [39] [Dataset-Specific Encoding of Image Content Underlies Cross-Dataset Failure in Brain MRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13027)
*Minh Sao Khue Luu,Bair N. Tuchinov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出脑部MRI基础模型的冻结特征在新数据集上失效的根源并非编码了数据集来源，而是同一内容在不同数据集中沿不同方向被编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为冻结的脑部MRI基础模型在新数据集上精度下降是因为特征编码了扫描来源（数据集信息）。作者对此提出质疑，希望重新定位失效的真正原因。

Method: 在17个公开数据集和8种冻结表示上进行实验，测试序列识别能力；使用INLP、LEACE、CORAL及对抗训练等方法去除数据集信号；比较3DINO、SAM-Med3D与脑专用模型（BrainIAC、BrainFM）及未训练网络的表现。

Result: 每种表示都能在单个数据集内识别MRI序列，但多数在未见数据集上失败。原因既非数据集编码也非各数据集自身的偏移，而是内容在不同数据集中沿不同方向编码。去除数据集信号的方法从未改善序列迁移。只有广泛预训练的3DINO和SAM-Med3D能一致编码并迁移；脑专用模型BrainIAC和BrainFM不优于未训练网络。

Conclusion: 失效根源在于内容表征方向的数据集依赖性，而非数据集来源编码或分布偏移，这解释了哪些数据集会失效，并说明脑专用基础模型在序列迁移上并无优势。

Abstract: Frozen features of brain MRI foundation models lose accuracy on new datasets, a failure usually blamed on the features encoding where each scan came from. We show that the failure lies elsewhere. Across 17 public datasets and eight frozen representations, every representation recognizes the MRI sequence within each dataset, yet most fail on an unseen one. The cause is neither dataset encoding nor a shift of each dataset: the content itself is encoded along different directions in each dataset. This explains which datasets fail, for the sequence and for sex, and why removing the dataset signal with INLP, LEACE, CORAL or adversarial training never improves sequence transfer. Only the broadly pretrained 3DINO and SAM-Med3D encode the sequence consistently enough to transfer; the brain-specific BrainIAC and BrainFM do no better than an untrained network. Code is available at https://github.com/BrainFM/brainfm-profiler/tree/main/representation_utility.

</details>


### [40] [GeoGR: Spatial-Semantic Token Compression and Group Routing for Efficient 3D VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13035)
*Yongxin Zheng,Hao Wu,Fang Li,Hu Fu,Peiran Yin,Xudong Wang,Haozhe Hu,Xinghao Chen,Yunpu Ma,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoGR是一个两阶段框架，通过联合压缩场景表示与视觉计算，大幅加速3D多视图视觉语言模型的LLM预填充并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图表示使预训练图像和视频视觉语言模型能够理解3D场景，但冗长的视觉token序列导致推理成本高昂。冗余来源于两个方面：跨视图对同一场景区域的重复观测，以及LLM每一层都处理所有视觉token而不管其与当前计算的相关性。

Method: 提出GeoGR两阶段框架。投影器层面，GeoSemZip将跨视图观测整合为3D体素token，结合语义显著性与空间覆盖构建紧凑场景表示，并将未选中的体素特征聚合为上下文锚点以保留信息。LLM层面，发现有效的视觉计算窗口，观察到查询条件的token相关性在局部稳定但跨较长层间隔变化，据此设计GroupRoute，结合晚进入、早退出与可恢复的分组跳过；低相关token跳过完整LLM层但保留隐藏状态，可在后续锚点层重新加入活跃组。压缩感知后训练方面，通过保留原始训练池30%构建67K样本的紧凑数据集。

Result: 该协议下，GeoGR在LLaVA-OneVision-7B和Video-3D LLM-7B上分别保留稠密后训练模型95.2%和96.8%的性能，同时将LLM预填充加速6.8倍和6.1倍。

Conclusion: 结果证明GeoGR在有无显式3D空间编码的3D VLM上均一致有效，展现了良好的性能—效率权衡。

Abstract: Multi-view representations enable pretrained image and video vision-language models to understand 3D scenes, but their long visual token sequences make inference costly. Redundancy arises both from repeated observations of the same scene regions across views and from processing all visual tokens at every LLM layer, regardless of their relevance to the current computation. We propose GeoGR, a two-stage framework that jointly compresses scene representations and visual computation. At the projector level, GeoSemZip consolidates cross-view observations into 3D voxel tokens and combines semantic saliency with spatial coverage to construct a compact scene representation. Unselected voxel features are aggregated into contextual anchors to retain scene information that would otherwise be discarded. At the LLM level, we identify an effective visual computation window and observe that query-conditioned token relevance remains locally stable but changes over longer layer intervals. Guided by these findings, GroupRoute combines late entry and early exit with recoverable group-wise skipping. Low-relevance tokens bypass complete LLM layers while retaining their hidden states and can rejoin the active group at subsequent anchor layers selected under a computation budget. For compression-aware post-training, we construct a compact dataset of 67K samples by retaining 30% of the original training pool. With this protocol, GeoGR retains 95.2% and 96.8% of the dense post-trained models' performance on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Video-3D LLM-7B, respectively, while accelerating LLM prefill by $6.8\times$ and $6.1\times$. These results demonstrate the consistent effectiveness of GeoGR across 3D VLMs with and without explicit 3D spatial encoding, highlighting a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/GeoGR.

</details>


### [41] [Redirecting Teacher Supervision: Temporal-Response-Guided Latent Distillation for Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13045)
*Xinkai Lu,Junli Zha,Haolan Wei,Jiahui Wang,Chao Zhou,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Temporal-Response Guidance (TRG)，一种利用学生模型对局部时间扰动的表征响应来确定监督方向的内部监督方法，在多个视频基准上提升了4B学生模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy distillation中，直接对学生隐状态进行对齐并未显式利用学生对时间响应的信息来选择监督方向，因此需要探索内部监督如何更好地支持时间学习。

Method: TRG 度量表征对局部时间扰动的响应，将教师修正在该响应子空间上的投影加回原修正，并恢复其原始范数；投影不单独归一化，响应空间外的修正分量予以保留；使用正交切投影与detached目标构造监督。

Result: 在构造目标时，TRG 在保持余弦损失值与对齐表征梯度范数不变的情况下改变了监督方向；在 TempCompass、Video-MME、MVBench 和 Video-MMMU 上，4B学生模型的宏平均精度较仅输出OPD基线提升2.86个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过响应引导的方式调整内部监督方向，可在不改变目标构造关键量的前提下有效提升视频时间学习的效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers capabilities through teacher predictions on student-generated sequences, as studied by GKD and MiniLLM for language models and Video-OPD for video tasks. We ask how internal supervision can further support temporal learning. Direct hidden-state alignment does not explicitly use the student's temporal responses to choose a supervision direction. We propose Temporal-Response Guidance (TRG), which measures representation responses to local temporal perturbations, adds the teacher correction's projection onto their span back to the original correction, and restores its original norm. The projection is not normalized separately, and correction components outside the response space are retained. With an orthogonal tangent projector and a detached target, TRG changes the supervision direction while preserving the cosine-loss value and aligned-representation gradient norm at target construction. On TempCompass, Video-MME, MVBench, and Video-MMMU, TRG improves the 4B student's macro accuracy by 2.86 percentage points over the reported output-only OPD baseline.

</details>


### [42] [NOFACE3D: NOise-scheduled Focused Anchoring and Controlled Enhancement for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13050)
*James Rose,Jingwen Liang,Wenchao Ma,Oren Jacob,Haomiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练框架 NOFACE3D，通过局部放大与重编码，细化单图生成3D资产中的细节关键区域，在多个数据集上取得更锐利的几何与纹理细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图到3D模型生成的资产整体形状与外观已较可信，但细小、细长或细节丰富区域因固定分辨率空间网格采用统一采样密度而被重建得模糊、欠采样，细节不足。

Method: 给定生成网格、输入图像与细化框，NOFACE3D 提取选定子网格，放大以填满生成器输入体积，重编码到全分辨率潜变量网格；以图像裁剪为条件，通过流式采样与空间变化的噪声调度（锚定边界潜变量）细化该区域，再融合回原资产，保持周边几何与外观。实现于 TRELLIS.2 及其稀疏体素表示，全部在推理时对冻结预训练生成器操作。

Result: 在 PAniC-3D、TexVerse 以及新基准 BigDetail（339个资产，带细节关键区域标注）上，NOFACE3D 生成的几何更锐利、纹理细节更精细，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: NOFACE3D 以免训练、推理期局部细化方式有效弥补固定分辨率生成器的细节不足问题，在保持整体资产不变的同时显著提升细节质量。

Abstract: Recent image-to-3D models generate complete, high-quality assets from a single image, with global shape and appearance that are already convincing; what remains is the fine scale, where small, thin, elongated, or detail-rich regions are reconstructed softly and under-resolved. This gap can arise in generators with fixed-resolution spatial grids: the same spatial sampling density is used for a large, smooth, low-curvature surface and for a thin, high-curvature structure that carries most of the perceived detail. To this end, we introduce NOFACE3D, a training-free framework that refines these detail-critical regions while preserving the rest of the asset. Given a generated mesh, its input image, and a refinement box, NOFACE3D extracts the selected submesh, scales it up to fill the generator's input volume, and re-encodes it into the full-resolution latent grid. Conditioned on the image crop, it refines the region through flow-based sampling with a spatially varying noise schedule that anchors boundary latents. The refined region is then integrated back into the original asset, preserving the surrounding geometry and appearance. We implement NOFACE3D on TRELLIS.2 and its sparse voxel representation; all of it operates purely at inference time on the frozen pretrained generator. We evaluate NOFACE3D on PAniC-3D, TexVerse and BigDetail, a new benchmark of 339 assets with annotated detail-critical regions. On all three, it generates sharper geometry and finer texture detail than the baselines. Project page: https://idkcouscous.github.io/NoFACE3D/

</details>


### [43] [UniMergeTrack: Hierarchical Model Merging for General RGB-X Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13053)
*Shiao Wang,Xiao Wang,Yuhe Ding,Jiarui Xu,Zikang Yan,Bo Jiang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMergeTrack 提出一种模型合并范式，先在多模态跟踪任务上独立训练，再通过分层合并实现跨任务知识整合，在多个 RGB-X 基准上达到领先或竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RGB-X 跟踪器存在两类范式：独立任务训练导致知识隔离与模型冗余；联合训练则面临异构任务间的优化干扰。论文旨在兼顾任务特定优化与跨任务知识整合。

Method: 先独立训练各模态专用跟踪器，再进行分层模型合并：用高通滤波合并跨 RGB-X 任务的可迁移骨干网络，并辅以轻量任务特定专家保留模态线索；在每个任务内用心最优传输引导的通道对齐合并跟踪头以缓解隐藏通道不匹配；最后通过分布对齐微调提升统一骨干与任务特定头的兼容性。

Result: 在 LasHeR、RGBT234、VisEvent、VOT-RGBD22、COESOT 和 CDTB 等基准上，UniMergeTrack 在 RGB-热红�、RGB-事件和 RGB-深度跟踪任务中取得领先或竞争力性能，并对未见数据集展现良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了模型合并范式在多模态跟踪中的有效性，能在保留任务特定优化的同时实现跨任务知识整合，代码将在接收后于指定 GitHub 仓库开源。

Abstract: RGB-X tracking leverages complementary modalities, such as thermal infrared, event, or depth, to improve robustness under challenging conditions. Existing general-purpose RGB-X trackers typically follow two paradigms: independent task-specific training and joint training across modality combinations. However, the former suffers from knowledge isolation and model redundancy, while the latter introduces optimization interference across heterogeneous tasks. In this work, we present UniMergeTrack, a new model merging paradigm that preserves task-specific optimization while enabling effective cross-task knowledge integration. UniMergeTrack first trains modality-specific trackers independently and then performs hierarchical model merging. Specifically, transferable backbones are merged across RGB-X tasks via high-pass-filtered merging, and complemented by lightweight task-specific experts that preserve modality-specific cues. Tracking heads are merged within each task using optimal transport-guided channel alignment to mitigate hidden channel mismatch. A subsequent distribution alignment fine-tuning stage further improves compatibility between the unified backbone and task-specific heads. Extensive experiments on the LasHeR, RGBT234, VisEvent, VOT-RGBD22, COESOT, and CDTB benchmarks demonstrate that UniMergeTrack achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance across RGB-Thermal, RGB-Event, and RGB-Depth tracking tasks, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen datasets. The source code will be released upon acceptance. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenEvTracking.

</details>

*Noel D'souza,Zhijian Yang,Nishank Singla,Pranay Doshi,Krisztian Koos,Fei Tan,Bruno Nunes,Laszlo Rusko,Bernadett Kolozsvari,Eszter Sabo,Emanuele Valeriano,Vikash Gupta,Seyed Iman Zare Estakhraji,Marc Lebel,Farzin Haddadpour,Taha Kass-Hout,Erhan Bas*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 解剖层面的多模态对齐与异常感知表征学习，可使单一MRI基础模型同时支撑定位、分割和病理相关任务，验证了该训练范式的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale foundation models have enabled strong generalization across a wide range of clinical and research tasks in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). However, existing vision-language MRI models remain limited in anatomy-specific localization and pathology-aware representation, particularly in real-world settings. We introduce A4-MR, a 3D vision-language foundation model trained on approximately 50,000 MRI studies comprising 491,000 series, roughly half of which are paired with radiology reports. A4-MR is designed to: (1) learn anatomy localization through large-scale pretraining with noisy labels; (2) align image and text representations at the anatomical level; and (3) learn distinct representations of healthy and abnormal anatomy. Across a diverse set of downstream tasks, A4-MR outperforms existing foundation models, including a +1.7 AUROC improvement over MedSigLIP for abnormality detection. Using a frozen encoder, A4-MR also surpasses an end-to-end task-specific MedNeXt model for knee segmentation by +1.4 Dice and performs comparably to the best task-specific model for abdominal organ segmentation on AMOS, within 0.4 Dice. Finally, without anatomical prompts, A4-MR localizes 3D anatomy comparably to MedSAM2 provided with ground-truth bounding boxes. Together, these results demonstrate that anatomy-level multimodal alignment and abnormality-aware representation learning can enable a single MRI foundation model to support localization, segmentation, and pathology-related tasks.

</details>


### [45] [Evolving Creatives with Clicks: CTR-Driven Advertising image Editing via Attribute Disentanglement and Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13066)
*Xu Zhang,Haohan Wang,Zheng Zhang,Yingjie Xia,Wei Feng,Honghe Zhu,Jingjing Lv,Changping Peng,Xuheng Qin,Zehua Zhang,Zheng Luo,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ECC方法，将电商广告创意优化重构为细粒度编辑而非从头生成，仅精修损害点击率的属性并保留有益属性，显著提升广告图片CTR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于CTR奖励信号生成广告图片，但对表现不佳图片中的所有属性一视同仁地惩罚，忽视了其中一些属性实际上对点击有益。因此需要一种能区分并保留有益属性、只修正有害属性的优化方式。

Method: ECC首先通过ANOVA与随机森林回归从海量图片中识别出28个与CTR显著相关的属性；再基于高/低CTR图片间的属性对比，用指令微调prompt模型生成编辑建议，并利用真实点击偏好进一步精修；同时提出一致偏好优化（CPO）策略，对语义等价的打乱建议施加一致性约束，以缓解DPO过拟合并提升偏好泛化。

Result: 大量实验表明，ECC显著提升了广告图片的CTR，并以较大幅度超越当前最先进模型。

Conclusion: 将创意优化建模为细粒度属性编辑并结合一致性偏好优化，能有效提升广告创意的点击表现；相关数据流程、数据集与源代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: The optimization objective for e-commerce advertising creatives has shifted from aesthetic quality to online business metrics like click-through rate (CTR). Recent methods generate appealing ad images from product images by constructing CTR-based reward signals and refining prompt generation via Reinforcement Learning. Such paradigms equally penalize all attributes within an underperforming image, even though some are actually beneficial to clicks. In this work, we propose Evolving Creatives with Clicks (ECC), which reformulates creative optimization as fine-grained editing rather than generation from scratch. ECC selectively refines attributes that hurt CTR while preserving those that contribute positively. Specifically, faced with a rich attribute space across massive images, ECC first identifies 28 attributes significantly correlated with CTR via ANOVA and Random Forest regression. Based on attribute contrasts between images with disparate CTRs, we instruction-tune a prompt model to generate editing suggestions, then refine it with real click preferences since reference-derived suggestions are not uniformly beneficial. Furthermore, we introduce Consistent Preference Optimization (CPO) as ECC's preference optimization strategy, which enforces consistency across semantically equivalent shuffled suggestions to mitigate DPO overfitting and improve preference generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ECC significantly improves the CTR of ad images, outperforming state-of-the-art models by a substantial margin. The complete data pipeline, including attribute extraction and analysis, the resulting dataset, and source code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [46] [Volume Rendering with Metric--Phase Fields for Thin Structures and Sign-Ambiguous Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13088)
*Junkai Deng,Jiayi Kong,Xuhui Chen,Guanli Hou,Fei Hou,Junhui Hou,Wenping Wang,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseSurf 提出基于 Metric-Phase Fields 的神经体渲染框架，将几何分解为非负度量场与连续相位场，兼顾 SDF 的稳定梯度与 UDF 对非水密几何的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将体渲染与 SDF 结合的方法依赖全局一致的符号内外划分，对薄结构（片、管、薄壁）和符号模糊几何（带边界曲面、非流形连接、近距离组件）易失效，常出现过闭合或细节丢失；而 UDF 虽放宽符号要求却难以优化。

Method: 提出 Metric-Phase Fields (MPFs)，把几何分解为非负度量场（无符号邻近性）与连续相位场（软符号提示），通过残差相位注入耦合，得到带稳定近表面梯度的符号复合隐函数，同时保持类似 UDF 的鲁棒性；设计 MPF 专用训练损失以强制度量一致性、稳定界面附近相位过渡、抑制自由空间伪几何。

Result: 在多视图数据集上（含薄结构与符号模糊几何），PhaseSurf 的重建比 UDF 类方法和近期 3DGS 流程更稳定、几何质量更高，同时避免 SDF 类方法典型伪影。

Conclusion: 该方法可直接用标准 Marching Cubes 提取等值面，规避纯 SDF 与纯 UDF 两类表述的失效模式，为非水密及符号模糊几何的高质量重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Neural implicit surface methods that couple volume rendering with signed distance functions (SDFs), such as VolSDF and NeuS, achieve high-quality multi-view reconstruction for watertight objects. However, these approaches rely on a globally consistent inside-outside partition of space. This assumption becomes fragile for thin structures (e.g., sheets, tubes, and thin walls) and can break down for sign-ambiguous geometry (e.g., surfaces with boundary, non-manifold junctions, and closely spaced surface components), often resulting in spurious closures or loss of fine structures. Unsigned distance field (UDF) formulations relax the global sign requirement and better support non-watertight geometry, but are substantially harder to optimize. We propose PhaseSurf, a neural volume rendering framework built on Metric-Phase Fields (MPFs). MPFs decompose geometry into a nonnegative metric field capturing unsigned proximity and a continuous phase field providing a soft sign cue. By coupling the two through residual phase injection, MPFs yield a signed composite implicit function with stable near-surface gradients while retaining UDF-like robustness in sign-ambiguous regions. This design enables direct iso-surface extraction using standard Marching Cubes and avoids the failure modes of both pure SDF- and UDF-based formulations. We further introduce MPF-specific training losses that enforce metric consistency, stabilize phase transitions near the interface, and suppress spurious geometry in free space. Experiments on multi-view datasets with thin structures and sign-ambiguous geometry show that PhaseSurf produces more stable reconstructions and higher-quality geometry than UDF-based methods and recent 3DGS pipelines, while avoiding artifacts typical of SDF-based approaches.

</details>


### [47] [Robust Vision-Language Navigation with Awareness of Instruction-Execution Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13104)
*Jiagan Huang,Zikun Zhou,Hongpeng Wang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLN-AIEP框架，将指令执行进度估计与导航决策耦合，通过地标锚定的轨迹表示抑制已执行指令内容，无需显式进度监督即可提升多步指令视觉语言导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多步指令引导的视觉语言导航（VLN）中，智能体需在每一步决策时将观测与相关指令内容关联。现有基于图的方法通常将候选轨迹与整条指令匹配，导致已执行内容干扰后续决策。尽管执行进度是分离已执行与待执行内容的直接线索，但已有进度估计方法依赖显式进度监督、复杂的进度学习阶段或昂贵的LLM在线推理。

Method: 提出VLN-AIEP框架，将进度估计与导航决策耦合：先将已访问轨迹与指令关联，抑制对应已执行内容的嵌入，再将候选位置与待执行内容匹配。该耦合使正确导航决策依赖准确进度估计，从而让进度估计器可直接从导航决策标签学习，无需显式进度标注。为支持进度估计，构建地标锚定的轨迹表示，编码指令所指地标的语义标签与视角相对几何关系，作为关联已访问轨迹与已执行指令内容的空间参照。

Result: 在R2R、RxR、CVDN和R2R-CE数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在多项指标上取得了良好性能。

Conclusion: VLN-AIEP通过耦合进度估计与导航决策，并利用地标锚定的轨迹表示，在无需显式进度监督的情况下有效缓解已执行指令干扰问题，在多个VLN基准上表现优异。

Abstract: In Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) tasks involving multi-step instructions as guidance, an agent must associate its observations with the relevant instruction content at each decision point. Most graph-based methods commonly match candidate trajectories with the entire instruction, allowing already executed content to interfere with subsequent decisions. Although instruction-execution progress provides a direct cue for separating executed and pending content, existing progress estimation approaches often rely on explicit progress supervision, sophisticated progress-learning stages, or costly online reasoning with LLMs. In this paper, we propose a robust VLN framework with Awareness of Instruction-Execution Progress (VLN-AIEP), which couples progress estimation with navigation decision-making. VLN-AIEP associates the visited trajectory with the instruction to suppress embeddings corresponding to executed content, and then matches candidate locations with the pending content. This coupling makes correct navigation decisions depend on accurate progress estimation, allowing the progress estimator to learn directly from navigation decision labels without explicit progress annotations. To support progress estimation, we construct a landmark-anchored trajectory representation that encodes the semantic labels and viewpoint-relative geometry of instruction-referred landmarks. These landmarks serve as spatial references for associating the visited trajectory with executed instruction content. Extensive experiments on R2R, RxR, CVDN, and R2R-CE demonstrate that the proposed method achieves favorable performance across multiple metrics.

</details>


### [48] [What Do Visual-Head Discovery Scores Actually Find? Attention Source, Necessity, and Random-Control Pitfalls in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13111)
*Md Abdul Kadir,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统检验了视觉-语言模型（VLM）中"视觉注意力头"发现流程内部设计选择（注意力来源、查询类型、语料）对所选头集合的影响，并用可操控性（CERS）与必要性（NCS）两种验证方式对12组头集合进行检验。结果发现必要性主要随注意力来源变化，而可操控性无法区分所发现的头集合与同层随机头集合，据此提出视觉头发现报告的规范协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注图像中与问题相关区域的注意力头被广泛用于引导、定位和解释VLM并减少幻觉，但这类头的发现通常依赖注意力模式打分排名，再通过操控或遮蔽来验证。然而，打分流程中的具体选择（如读取哪些token位置的注意力、使用何种查询类型、在什么语料上发现）如何影响最终返回的头集合，以及两种验证方式中哪一种真正能识别出这些头，尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清这些设计选择的作用，并给出可复现的发现协议。

Method: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上采用完整的2×3×2设计，变化三个因素：查询类型、发现语料、读取注意力的token位置（注意力来源）。对由此得到的12组头集合，分别用CERS（对比证据区域引导分数）检验可操控性，用NCS（必要性对比分数）检验必要性，并与层匹配的随机头集合进行对比。此外在多个近期VLM上复现关键效应。

Result: （1）注意力来源与查询类型对所选头的重塑远大于语料：改变来源时top-100头中位数保留49个，查询为55个，语料为84个，而同一数据的两半之间共享96-99个。（2）必要性跟随注意力来源：基于查询跨度注意力的头集合比末token的更具必要性（差异0.083，p=6×10⁻¹⁰），两个指代+查询跨度集合比250个层匹配随机集合高出17-19个标准差（Holm校正p=0.048）；该效应在多个近期VLM上复现。（3）可操控性对所发现的头不具特异性：没有任何发现头集合比其层匹配随机集合操控得更好（排名p≥0.14），来自同层或任意层的随机集合的分数覆盖了发现集合的分数范围。

Conclusion: 必要性（NCS）能够区分不同的发现设置，而当前强度下的可操控性（CERS）不能。因此作者将上述发现转化为一套报告视觉头发现的协议，建议研究者明确报告注意力来源、查询类型与语料等设计选择，并以必要性作为更可靠的验证标准。

Abstract: Attention heads that attend to image regions relevant to a question are increasingly used to steer, ground and interpret vision-language models (VLMs) and to reduce their hallucinations. They are mostly found by ranking heads with an attention- pattern score and validated by steering or masking them. We ask how the choices inside such a discovery score shape the heads it returns, and which of the two validations identifies them. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct we vary the query type, the discovery corpus and the token positions whose attention is read (the attention source) in a full 2 x 3 x 2 design, and test all 12 head-sets for steerability (CERS: Contrastive Evidence-Region Steering score) and necessity (NCS: Necessity Con- trast Score) against layer-matched random head-sets. (1) The attention source and the query type reshape the selected heads far more than the corpus: changing the source keeps a median of 49 of the top-100 heads, the query 55 and the cor- pus 84, while two halves of the same data share 96-99. (2) Necessity follows the attention source: head-sets discovered from query-span attention are more nec- essary than last-token ones (difference 0.083, p=6 x 10^-10), and the two re- ferring + query-span sets lie 17-19 standard deviations above 250 layer-matched random sets (Holm-adjusted p=0.048). The referring + query-span effect re- plicates on several recent VLMs. (3) Steerability is not specific to the discovered heads: no discovered head-set steers better than its layer-matched random sets (rank p >= 0.14), and random sets drawn from the same or from any layer span the discovered sets' scores. Necessity therefore separates the discovery settings, while steering at this strength does not. We turn these findings into a protocol for reporting visual-head discoveries.

</details>


### [49] [Replaying the Movie in Your Mind Decoding Dynamic Visual Perception and Recall from EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13120)
*Zikai Fang,Tian-Yi Zhou,Xuan-Hao Liu,Yixi Li,Xiaoyu Xing,Yutong Pei,Bao-Liang Lu,Wei-Long Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PAIR——一个包含20名被试的EEG基准数据集，采用交替、片段式范式将自然视频观看与即时视觉回忆配对，用于比较同一刺激内容在外部感知与内部回忆下的神经表征，并评测了多种解码模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态视觉内容的EEG解码在视觉感知方面进展迅速，但人们内部重放动态场景时，神经表征中究竟保留了哪些方面仍不清楚。缺乏在同一试次序列中直接对比感知与回忆的配对数据集与基准。

Method: 构建PAIR数据集：20名被试，采用交替、按片段（clip-wise）的范式，将自然视频观看与紧随其后的视觉回忆配对，从而在固定试次序列内比较同一刺激内容的外驱感知与内驱回忆。评测多类解码模型，任务包括细粒度与粗粒度层级语义分类、涵盖运动、颜色、物体数量、人脸与人存在的元特征解码，以及区分感知与回忆的状态解码。

Result: 被试内验证显示，若干模型—任务组合的性能在数值上相近；经多重比较校正后，两项粗粒度比较中感知准确率更高。相比之下，在当前验证协议下，感知与回忆在状态层面高度可分。去除alpha频带后状态准确率仍保持较高，但并不能排除眼动或其他任务相关因素的贡献。

Conclusion: 感知与回忆在语义与元特征层面的神经表征具有一定相似性，但在状态层面高度可分；alpha频带并非状态区分的主要来源，其贡献仍需结合眼动等因素进一步厘清。

Abstract: Decoding dynamic visual content from EEG has advanced rapidly for visual perception, yet it remains unclear which aspects of neural representations are preserved when people internally replay dynamic scenes. We introduce PAIR, an EEG benchmark collected from 20 participants using an interleaved, clip-wise paradigm that pairs naturalistic video viewing with immediate visual recall. This design enables a comparison between externally driven perception and internally generated recall for the same stimulus content within a fixed trial sequence. We benchmark multiple decoding models on hierarchical semantic classification at both fine-grained and coarse-grained levels, meta-feature decoding tasks covering motion, color, object number, face, and human presence, and a state-decoding task that discriminates perception from recall. Within-subject validation results show numerically similar performance for several model--task combinations, alongside higher perception accuracy in two coarse-grained comparisons after multiple-comparison correction. In contrast, perception and recall are highly separable at the state level under the present validation protocol. State accuracy remains high after alpha-band ablation, although this does not exclude ocular or other task-related contributions.

</details>


### [50] [GuidedNav: Shaping Vision-Language Navigation Representations through Pre-action Attention and Spatial Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13122)
*Jingyu Guo,Jiaxin Huang,Chengxing Lin,Xiaoyu Luo,Cheng Wen,Shanshan Ye,Tingjin Chu,Shaoli Huang,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GuidedNav 通过在训练时对指令-动作之间的表征进行显式引导（子任务、地标与空间几何），在不增加推理开销的情况下提升 VLN 泛化能力，并在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉语言导航策略通常仅依赖动作监督隐式学习能力，缺乏对决策依据的显式指导，容易过拟合与训练动作相关的伪相关，而非真正学习任务所需的地标定位与空间几何理解，从而损害泛化能力。

Method: 提出 GuidedNav 统一框架，在训练阶段显式塑造连接指令与动作的表征：子任务与地标引导将原生预动作注意力导向当前指令片段与相关视觉区域；空间引导使策略内部表征对齐几何特征，以捕获长程上下文与局部空间结构。同时构建 Guided-R2R 与 Guided-RxR 两个标注增强数据集。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE val-unseen 上，相较最强先前结果成功率分别相对提升 8.1% 与 13.1%，且在所比较的单目 RGB 策略中达到最优，并实现更快的推理速度；实验还验证了方法的有效性与真实场景迁移能力。

Conclusion: 显式引导指令-动作表征可在无中间文本推理与在线几何编码开销的前提下增强导航能力，为更具可解释性的 VLN 研究提供数据集与框架支持。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to track progress through an extended instruction, identify landmarks relevant to its current objective, and understand the surrounding spatial structure to act. End-to-end navigation policies typically rely on action supervision to implicitly learn these capabilities. Without explicit guidance on the evidence underlying each decision, however, policies can overfit to correlations predictive of training actions rather than learn the task-relevant grounding and geometry needed for generalization. Our insight is to guide the representations that connect instructions to actions: identifying what remains to be done determines which visual evidence matters, while understanding the geometry around that evidence informs how to move. We introduce GuidedNav, a unified framework that explicitly shapes these representations through training-time guidance. Subtask and landmark guidance steer native pre-action attention toward the active instruction segment and relevant visual regions, grounding action prediction in the current objective. Spatial guidance aligns the policy's internal representations with geometric features that capture long-horizon context and local spatial structure. Together, these objectives strengthen navigation capabilities while avoiding the inference overhead of intermediate textual reasoning and online geometric encoding. We also introduce Guided-R2R and Guided-RxR, two annotation-enriched navigation datasets that support more interpretable VLN research. GuidedNav establishes state-of-the-art performance among the compared monocular RGB policies, improving success rates by 8.1% and 13.1% relative to the strongest prior results on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, respectively, while achieving faster inference. Extensive experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness and real-world transferability of our approach.

</details>


### [51] [Measurable Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting with Joint Region and Depth Supervision for Interactive Building Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13143)
*Yi Zhang,Tian Tan,Ray Kai Leung Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可测量的语义3D高斯泼溅框架，结合区域与深度监督，实现建筑巡检的可测量、语义化与交互式表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的三维重建虽可提供可扩展表示，但仅靠逼真重建无法满足工程应用所需的几何可测量性、语义结构和交互式信息访问。

Method: 在3DGS中引入掩膜区域监督以抑制背景、聚焦目标区域；利用MVS点云作为参考几何进行逐点深度监督，引导高斯中心贴近物理表面；构建语义高斯场，将可学习类别特征绑定到各高斯基元，实现语义渲染与构件级检查；并通过AEC-SplatTwin平台实现实时可视化、语义分析、量化测量与MLLM辅助交互。

Result: 在QuakeCity与HKUPB数据集上，高斯中心深度MAE分别降低58.1%和86.8%；语义场mIoU达0.8529和0.8642；HKUPB上高度、宽度、面积测量的平均相对误差分别为0.33%、1.07%、0.60%。

Conclusion: 该框架将3DGS从视觉重建拓展为可测量、语义结构化且可交互的工程表示，适用于建筑巡检。

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction provides a scalable representation for building inspection and maintenance. However, photorealistic reconstruction alone does not fully provide the geometric measurability, semantic structure, and interactive information access required for engineering applications. This study proposes a measurable semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) framework with region and depth supervision for interactive building inspection from multi-view imagery. Mask-based region supervision suppresses irrelevant backgrounds and focuses optimization on target regions. Point-wise depth supervision guides Gaussian centers toward physical surfaces using reference geometry derived from multi-view stereo (MVS) point clouds, providing geometric anchors for measuring physical dimensions and areas. A semantic Gaussian field associates learnable class features with individual Gaussian primitives, enabling semantic rendering and component-level inspection. These capabilities are operationalized through AEC-SplatTwin, an interactive building inspection platform supporting real-time visualization, semantic analysis, quantitative measurement, and multimodal large language model (MLLM)-assisted interaction. On the synthetic UAV-inspection QuakeCity and smartphone-captured HKUPB datasets, the framework reduces Gaussian-center depth MAE by 58.1% and 86.8%, while its semantic field achieves mIoU values of 0.8529 and 0.8642. On HKUPB, the mean relative errors for height, width, and area measurements are 0.33%, 1.07%, and 0.60%. These results show that the proposed framework extends 3DGS beyond visual reconstruction into a measurable, semantically structured, and interactive engineering representation for building inspection.

</details>


### [52] [From Glance to Focus: Mitigating Recognition Laziness in MLLMs for Fine-Grained Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13154)
*Yutong Yang,Zhuqi Liu,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现MLLM在细粒度视觉识别中存在"FGVR Laziness"现象（视觉线索与类别概念关联不足），提出免训练框架LIVE，通过优化候选类别列表与识别引导来激发其潜在细粒度识别能力，在13个基准、24种方法对比中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型虽具备视觉感知与广泛类别知识等细粒度识别基础，但实际表现不佳。现有方法多依赖领域特定的后训练来弥补，却牺牲了对未见过域的泛化能力。作者从经验与理论角度重新审视MLLM的细粒度识别机制，试图在不训练的前提下解决问题。

Method: 提出免训练框架LIVE（Laziness-resIstant Visual rEcognition），联合优化候选列表与识别引导：一是查询自适应的候选推断，得到紧凑可靠的候选类别列表；二是通过挖掘类内特征与类间差异构建类别专属的细粒度识别技能；三是依据识别不确定性与类别混淆程度，选择性地调用详细技能引导，使模型从粗略一瞥转向将判别性视觉线索与类别概念关联。

Result: 在13个基准上与24种最先进方法对比，覆盖细粒度类别识别、动作识别与通用类别识别任务，验证了LIVE在不同MLLM骨干上的有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: MLLM的细粒度识别短板可通过推理阶段的候选列表与引导优化加以缓解，无需领域后训练即可激活其潜在能力，从而兼顾识别性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) generally fall short of expectations in Fine-Grained Visual Recognition (FGVR), although they exhibit promising FGVR foundations, i.e., visual perception and broad category knowledge. Most existing studies seek to remedy this deficiency by adopting domain-specific post-training, at the risk of compromising generalization to unseen domains. Different from these efforts, in this paper, we revisit the FGVR mechanism underlying MLLMs from both the empirical and theoretical perspectives and uncover an inference-time phenomenon termed FGVR Laziness. In brief, FGVR Laziness refers to the insufficient association between visual cues and category concepts under certain candidate category lists and recognition guidance, leaving the FGVR foundations of MLLMs largely under-utilized. To mitigate FGVR Laziness, we propose Laziness-resIstant Visual rEcognition (LIVE), a training-free framework which jointly refines candidate lists and recognition guidance to elicit the latent FGVR capability of MLLMs. Specifically, LIVE first performs query-adaptive candidate inference to derive a compact yet reliable candidate list. Meanwhile, LIVE constructs category-specific FGVR skills by mining the intra-category characteristics and inter-category differences. Finally, LIVE selectively invokes detailed skill guidance according to recognition uncertainty and category confusion, shifting MLLM-based FGVR from a cursory glance to a focus on associating discriminative visual cues with category concepts. Extensive experiments on 13 benchmarks against 24 state-of-the-art methods spanning fine-grained category recognition, action recognition, and general category recognition, verify the effectiveness and generalizability of LIVE across different MLLM backbones. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [53] [PreCoG: Preview-Edit for Efficient Video Correction in Generation-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13174)
*Yilai Liu,Wenqi Pei,Zhifeng Lu,Shiyuan Zhang,Zongxi Liu,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreCoG是一个迭代文本到视频生成框架，通过低成本预览诊断语义失误并编辑缓存轨迹进行校正，减少重复计算，在VBench和T2V-CompBench上取得最高分，且推理时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代文本到视频生成方法在每次校正时都需要完整重跑一次生成，导致重复计算，并可能破坏原本正确的内容，因此需要一种更高效且不干扰正确内容的校正方案。

Method: 提出PreCoG框架，包含PreQA和PreEdit两个模块：PreQA从低成本预览中诊断语义失误并构建校正条件；PreEdit在这些条件下编辑缓存的生成轨迹，抑制正交变化以限制对已正确内容的破坏。生成仅针对选定候选完成，并复用其缓存轨迹。

Result: 在VBench和T2V-CompBench上取得最高分，接近VSR领先的VS2分数且推理时间为0.56倍；在三个质量和对齐指标上均优于GENMAC，推理时间为0.86倍。

Conclusion: PreCoG通过预览诊断与缓存轨迹编辑，实现了高效且一致的迭代视频生成校正，在保持生成质量的同时显著降低推理开销。

Abstract: Iterative text-to-video generation relies on feedback to correct compositional and temporal failures. However, existing methods typically require another full generation run for each correction, repeating computation and potentially disrupting already correct content. We introduce PreCoG, an iterative framework for correcting semantic failures during video generation. PreQA diagnoses failures from low-cost previews and constructs corrective conditions, while PreEdit edits cached generation trajectories under these conditions, attenuating orthogonal changes to limit disruption of already correct content. PreCoG completes generation only for the selected candidate, reusing its cached trajectory to reduce redundant computation and help preserve semantic consistency between the preview and the final video. Experiments show that PreCoG achieves the highest VBench and T2V-CompBench scores and approaches VSR's leading VS2 score at $0.56\times$ its inference time. It also outperforms GENMAC on all three quality and alignment metrics at $0.86\times$ its inference time.

</details>


### [54] [Fix the Match, Not the Solver: STITCH -- a Self-Supervised Compatibility Measure for Eroded Visual Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13176)
*Nadav Alali,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出现实碎片因边界侵蚀导致传统兼容性度量失效，并提出自监督兼容性度量STITCH，通过比较碎片内部冻结的ViT patch token来绕开缺失边界，在多个拼图基准上大幅刷新性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准拼图基准中的碎片干净且像素对齐，而现实碎片边界区域会因侵蚀而丢失，破坏了经典兼容性度量所依赖的信号。作者还通过oracle实验重新确认：性能瓶颈在于兼容性度量本身，而非求解器——只要度量完美，连简单贪心求解器也能完美重建。

Method: 提出STITCH，一种自监督兼容性度量。它不依赖缺失的边界，而是通过轻量级交叉注意力头（约249K可训练参数）比较碎片内部冻结的ViT patch token，无需人工标注即可训练，并可直接接入不改动的基于兼容性的求解器。

Result: 在JPwLEG-5上达到.992/.954 Abs/Perf，较最佳先前方法提升+.629 Perf，几乎饱和该基准；在新的ImageNet 7x7基准上优于所有替代方案，包括基于现代生成模型的先修复再匹配流程；在不同侵蚀程度下保持稳健，并可泛化到碎片缺失超过40%的拼图而无需重训；在GAP-3和GAP-5上，基于该度量配方的求解器超越了近期学习型求解器。

Conclusion: 性能的大幅跃升来自一个可插拔的兼容性度量模块，因此核心结论是：修复匹配（度量），而非求解器。

Abstract: Unlike the clean and pixel-aligned complete pieces of standard jigsaw benchmarks, real-world puzzle fragments lose their boundary regions to erosion, destroying the signal that classical compatibility measures (CMs) rely on. We first re-establish, via oracle experiments, that the CM, not the solver, is the performance bottleneck: with a perfect CM, even a simple greedy solver achieves perfect reconstruction. This motivates STITCH, a self-supervised CM that sidesteps missing boundaries by comparing frozen ViT patch tokens from piece interiors via a lightweight cross-attention head (~249K trainable parameters), trained without manual annotation. Plugged into unchanged compatibility-based solvers, STITCH reaches .992/.954 Abs/Perf on JPwLEG-5 (+.629 Perf over the best prior method), nearly saturating the benchmark. On a new ImageNet 7x7 benchmark, it outperforms all alternatives, including inpaint-then-match pipelines built on modern generative models. Importantly, performance remains robust across erosion levels and generalizes to puzzles with over 40% missing pieces without retraining. Trained on the recent GAP datasets of irregular, heavily eroded fragments, the same CM recipe enables compatibility-based solvers to surpass recent learning-based solvers on GAP-3 and GAP-5. Since this leap in performance is achieved by a pluggable CM module, the takeaway is clear: fix the match, not the solver.

</details>


### [55] [CompactSplat: Spatially Adaptive Gaussian Distribution for Feedforward Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13177)
*Bing He,Jingnan Gao,Yunuo Chen,Zhengxue Cheng,Rong Xie,Li Song,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CompactSplat，一种内容感知分配3D高斯的前馈框架，通过纹理引导空间划分与层次采样，在稀疏视角下实现高保真重建并显著减少图元数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法通常为每个像素分配一个或多个高斯，导致均匀分配产生高度冗余表示，在平坦区域浪费图元，且未利用平面表面的几何先验，结构建模欠佳。稀疏视角下无逐场景优化的3D场景重建仍具挑战。

Method: CompactSplat整合纹理引导的空间划分与层次采样，实现3D高斯的内容感知分配：在复杂区域集中稠密高斯，在平坦区域用扩展的尺度调制图元铺贴。框架天然支持推理时的动态稀疏控制。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、DL3DV和ScanNet上的大量实验表明，CompactSplat在标准指标和高分辨率渲染一致性上均优于先前方法，以显著更少的图元实现高保真重建。

Conclusion: CompactSplat 通过内容感知的高斯分配，有效解决前馈3DGS的冗余问题，并支持推理时的保真度与内存权衡，无需重新训练网络。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes from sparse views without per-scene optimization remains highly challenging, especially for recovering accurate geometry and fine textures. While recent feedforward approaches leverage generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for scene generation, they typically assign one or multiple Gaussians to each pixel. Such uniform allocation not only produces highly redundant representations by wasting primitives in homogeneous areas, but also fails to exploit the inherent geometric priors of planar surfaces, often resulting in sub-optimal structural modeling. To address these limitations, we present CompactSplat, a novel feedforward framework that enables a content-aware allocation of 3D Gaussians. By integrating texture-guided spatial partitioning with hierarchical sampling, CompactSplat adaptively distributes primitives - concentrating dense Gaussians in complex regions while tiling flat areas with expanded, scale-modulated primitives. Moreover, our framework inherently supports dynamic sparsity control at inference time, enabling seamless trade-offs between rendering fidelity and memory footprint without requiring network retraining. Extensive experiments on RealEstate10K, DL3DV, and ScanNet demonstrate that CompactSplat consistently outperforms prior methods on both standard metrics and high-resolution rendering consistency, achieving high-fidelity reconstructions with significantly fewer primitives.

</details>


### [56] [High-Throughput Generative Video Super-Resolution on a Single Consumer GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13179)
*Georgios Panaretou,Sotirios Chatzis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本生成式视频超分框架，由预训练组件构建，在单张RTX 4090上实现720p约32 FPS，并通过分辨率感知混合注意力在4K下最高获得44%加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频超分虽能借助扩散先验恢复高频细节，但迭代采样与沉重的时空耦合使其难以实际部署，作者希望在单张高端消费级GPU上实现免端到端重训的实用HD生成式VSR。

Method: 将空间增强与时间稳定解耦：用时间蒸馏的一步扩散骨干做空间上采样，用轻量预训练光流插帧器在超分关键帧间合成中间帧以提升时序连贯；并引入分辨率感知混合注意力，零样本地把部分高分辨率全局注意力层替换为邻域注意力，在语义关键的低分辨率层保留全局注意力。

Result: 在交错推理下，720p可达约32 FPS（单张NVIDIA RTX 4090）；4K下注意力替换相比原始实现最高提速44%。在标准VSR基准上，方法把速度-质量权衡显著推向运行效率，牺牲重建保真度换取较强的无参考感知质量，时序一致性上与生成式方法大致可比；对更困难运动存在明显局限，简单运动门控可部分缓解。

Conclusion: 该零样本框架证明无需端到端重训即可在消费级GPU上实现实用的HD生成式VSR，兼顾效率与感知质量，但困难运动场景仍是主要瓶颈，代码将在接收后释出。

Abstract: Generative video super-resolution (VSR) can recover visually compelling high-frequency detail by leveraging diffusion priors, but iterative sampling and heavy spatio-temporal coupling make practical deployments difficult. We study a different operating point: practical HD generative VSR on a single high-end consumer GPU without end-to-end retraining. To this end, we present a zero-shot framework built entirely from pretrained components.
  The proposed system decouples spatial enhancement from temporal stabilization. A time-distilled one-step diffusion backbone handles spatial upscaling, while temporal coherence is promoted by a lightweight pretrained flow-based interpolator that synthesizes intermediate frames between super-resolved keyframes. To further improve scalability, we introduce a resolution-aware hybrid attention scheme that, in a zero-shot manner, replaces selected high-resolution global-attention layers with neighborhood attention while preserving global attention in semantically critical low-resolution layers, while also improving temporal consistency.
  Experiments show that the proposed framework reaches up to ~32 FPS at 720p resolution under interleaved inference on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090. At 4K, the proposed attention substitution can yield up to a 44% speedup over the original attention implementation. Across standard VSR benchmarks, the method consistently shifts the speed-quality trade-off decisively toward runtime efficiency, trading reconstruction fidelity for strong no-reference perceptual quality, while remaining reasonably competitive against generative approaches in temporal consistency. It also exposes clear limitations in handling more challenging motion, which a simple motion gate partially mitigates. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [57] [MixTok: Adaptive Visual Tokenization with Global 1D Tokens and Sparse 2D Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13181)
*Chenge Li,Yefei He,Xinyu Liu,Feng Chen,Hong Zhou,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixTok是一种混合视觉分词器，用紧凑的1D全局表示配合稀疏2D局部token，通过内容自适应路由器在平均token预算下分配2D token以精修局部细节，在ImageNet 256×256上取得竞争性的重建与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网格型分词器对空间位置均匀分配token，忽略了视觉复杂度的非均匀性；而紧凑的1D分词器虽能聚合全局信息，却难以保留局部细节。因此需要兼顾表示紧凑性与细粒度内容保真度。

Method: 提出MixTok混合分词器：（1）结合紧凑1D全局表示与稀疏2D局部token；（2）内容自适应路由器对候选位置按精修优先级排序，并在目标平均token预算下决定每张图像的2D token数量；（3）在得分最高的位置用2D token特征替换从1D表示恢复的空间特征；（4）共享解码器从混合特征重建图像。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，平均预算约128个内容token时rFID为1.195，约131个内容token时gFID为1.95，重建与生成性能具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 内容自适应的1D+2D混合token化能够在受限的平均token预算下有效兼顾紧凑性与局部细节，验证了按视觉复杂度动态分配token的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient visual tokenization requires balancing representation compactness with the preservation of fine-grained image content. Conventional grid-based tokenizers allocate tokens uniformly across spatial locations despite non-uniform visual complexity, whereas compact 1D tokenizers aggregate global information but can struggle to preserve local details. We introduce MixTok, a hybrid visual tokenizer that combines a compact 1D global representation with sparse 2D tokens for local refinement. A content-adaptive router ranks candidate locations by refinement priority and determines the number of 2D tokens for each image under a target average token budget. At the highest-scoring locations, 2D token features replace the spatial features recovered from the 1D representation, and a shared decoder reconstructs the image from the resulting mixed features. On ImageNet 256 x 256, MixTok achieves highly competitive reconstruction and generation performance, with an rFID of 1.195 and a gFID of 1.95 at respective average budgets of approximately 128 and 131 content tokens per image.

</details>


### [58] [Destination-Conditioned Adaptation with Semantic-Geometric Alignment for Generative Inbetweening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13187)
*Zhiyu Xu,Wei Shang,Yushen Xu,Wenwen Li,Zhiyan Xu,Pengfei Zhu,Dongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DSG-GI框架，通过目标条件适配与语义-几何对齐，改进预训练潜视频扩散模型在生成式中间帧任务中的目标帧忠实度与时序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练图像到视频扩散模型虽为大运动中间帧生成提供了强先验，但未显式设计以到达指定目标帧，导致可能生成看似合理却偏离目标或不稳定的运动，尤其是在大位移和非刚体动态下。

Method: DSG-GI包含两部分：Destination-Conditioned Adaptation将目标帧视为序列终点，沿生成轨迹提供时间渐进式引导；Semantic-Geometric Alignment将主干特征的逐token时空关系几何与冻结视频基础编码器对齐，并对接近概率路径数据端点的样本施加流匹配感知的潜流约束。

Result: 在标准与大运动中间帧基准上的大量实验表明，方法在时序稳定性、目标帧忠实度和感知质量方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架通过增强目标引导并保持语义-几何运动结构一致性，有效适配预训练潜视频扩散模型，实现更可靠的生成式中间帧，代码将公开。

Abstract: Generative inbetweening aims to synthesize temporally coherent intermediate frames between a start frame and a target frame. Although pretrained image-to-video diffusion models provide strong priors for large-motion inbetweening, they are not explicitly designed to reach a specified target frame. They may therefore generate realistic individual frames while drifting from the target or producing unstable motion under large displacements and non-rigid dynamics. We propose DSG-GI, a framework that adapts pretrained latent video diffusion models through \textbf{D}estination-Conditioned Adaptation and \textbf{S}emantic-\textbf{G}eometric Alignment. Destination-Conditioned Adaptation treats the target frame as the destination of the sequence and provides temporally progressive guidance along the generated trajectory. Semantic-Geometric Alignment aligns the token-wise spatio-temporal relational geometry of backbone features with that of a frozen video foundation encoder and applies a flow-matching-aware latent flow constraint to samples near the data endpoint of the probability path. Together, these components strengthen destination guidance and preserve coherent semantic-geometric motion structure. Extensive experiments on standard and large-motion inbetweening benchmarks demonstrate improvements in temporal stability, target-frame faithfulness, and perceptual quality. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [59] [Golden Anchor for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13217)
*Jinyuan Xu,Shitong Shao,Lichen Bai,Zikai Zhou,Guoxi Huang,Nantheera Anantrasirichai,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出T2I生成的"锚点图像"并不天然适合下游I2V视频生成，并提出Golden Anchor Learning（GAL）框架，用下游视频反馈优化T2I生成器以产出"黄金锚点"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前I2V视频生成依赖T2I生成的锚点图像决定身份、构图与风格，但鲜有人质疑这些图像是否真正适合视频模型。作者发现锚点的美学与质量分数同下游视频质量弱相关，结构与纹理分数甚至与I2V视觉和运动质量负相关；用图像奖励微调T2I虽提升图像指标，却降低各I2V模型的视频奖励且运动变化不可控。

Method: 提出GAL框架：在冻结的I2V模型下用候选锚点生成视频，通过视频奖励评估锚点，并把视频奖励转化为图像级偏好，用DPO对T2I生成器进行微调，从而学习"黄金锚点"。

Result: 在四个I2V模型上，GAL使VBench平均分较未调优生成器提升2.97%–8.46%；渲染片段在VideoReward-Overall、抽取帧在HPSv3与UniPercept上得分更高；动态度提升5.62%–38.24%；仅在HunyuanVideo-1.5上训练，增益即可迁移到其他三个I2V模型。

Conclusion: 锚点图像应以视频反馈而非图像指标来优化；GAL是首个用下游视频反馈学习黄金锚点的框架，能一致提升多个I2V模型的视频质量与运动表现，并具备跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Video generation supports digital content creation, with applications in virtual avatars, AI-driven filmmaking, interactive game engines, and world models. State-of-the-art systems achieve controllability primarily through image-to-video (I2V) generation, in which an injected anchor image determines the identity, composition, and style of the resulting video. In modern pipelines, this anchor is synthesized by a text-to-image (T2I) generator. This raises a largely overlooked question: Are images obtained directly from the T2I model truly suitable for current video models? Our analysis shows that anchor aesthetic and quality scores are weakly correlated with downstream video quality. The structure-and-texture score is negatively correlated with I2V video quality in both the visual and motion dimensions. Fine-tuning the T2I model on an image reward accordingly yields anchors with visibly denser structural and character detail: they score higher on image metrics, yet lower the video reward on every I2V model we test and change downstream motion unpredictably. We therefore introduce Golden Anchor Learning (GAL), to our knowledge the first framework for learning golden anchors by optimizing a T2I generator with downstream video feedback. GAL evaluates candidate anchors through the videos they induce under a frozen I2V model and converts video rewards into image-level preferences for DPO fine-tuning of the T2I generator. On all four I2V models, GAL raises the VBench average by $2.97\%$--$8.46\%$ over the untuned generator. The rendered clips also score higher under VideoReward-Overall, and their extracted frames under HPSv3 and UniPercept. GAL yields consistent motion gains across all four I2V models, as measured by a $5.62\%$--$38.24\%$ increase in VBench dynamic degree. Although GAL is trained only on HunyuanVideo-1.5 renders, these gains extend to the other three I2V models.

</details>


### [60] [Complexity-Calibrated Reconstruction Discrepancy for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13219)
*Haocheng Wu,Xiangjie Sui,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参考的点云质量评估（PCQA）策略，通过连续重建估计并补偿内容复杂度引起的差异，最终融合几何与投影视图的校准差异得到质量分数，无需PCQA专用训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面重建在失真点云上产生的差异，同时受其内在结构复杂度和信号退化两方面影响。为准确评估质量，需要将复杂度引起的成分单独估计并加以补偿。

Method: 通过连续重建实现：重建失真点云得到"输入到重建"差异（混合退化与复杂度效应）；再用更强平滑的重建算子得到"前后两次重建"差异作为复杂度代理；用该代理校准前者。将此策略同时应用于3D几何块及其对齐的2D区域，进行局部差异比较。几何与投影视图的校准差异被融合为最终退化分数，差异均用冻结的预训练感知表示度量，无需PCQA专用训练或微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据库上的实验表明，该方法一致优于大多数通用基线，并与若干全参考指标性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过连续重建与复杂度校准，可在无需参考和专用训练的情况下有效评估点云质量，兼顾几何与投影视图信息，性能接近部分全参考方法。

Abstract: The discrepancy induced by surface reconstruction in a distorted point cloud is jointly influenced by its intrinsic structural complexity and signal degradation. This observation motivates a referencefree point cloud quality assessment (PCQA) strategy that estimates and compensates for the complexity-induced component. We implement this strategy through successive reconstruction. Specifically, reconstructing the distorted point cloud produces an input-toreconstruction discrepancy that combines the effects of signal degradation and content complexity. Reapplying the reconstruction operator with stronger smoothing produces a discrepancy between successive reconstructions, which serves as a proxy for the complexityinduced component. This proxy is then used to calibrate the inputto-reconstruction discrepancy. We apply the same strategy to 3D geometry patches and their aligned 2D regions, enabling local comparison of the resulting discrepancies. The calibrated geometric and projected-view discrepancies are then fused into a final degradation score. For both branches, the discrepancies are measured using frozen pretrained perceptual representations. Our method does not require PCQA-specific training or fine-tuning. Experiments on three public databases show that the proposed method consistently outperforms most generic baselines and achieves performance comparable to several full-reference metrics.The code is available at https://github.com/HC-W-whc/CCRD-NR-PCQA.git.

</details>


### [61] [Multi-Modal Graph Neural Network-Inspired Vessel Identification and Tracking for UAV-Based Maritime Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13220)
*Chenjie Zhao,Jiangtian Nie,Jiawen Kang,Yuxu Lu,Ryan Wen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AMGT框架，用于无人机辅助海事物联网中的船舶识别与跟踪，通过多模态对齐融合、AIS同步、检测跟踪算法及图学习关联网络，在MAP-M2UAV50数据集上实现高精度与高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机海事监控成为海事物联网与智能交通系统的重要组成，能灵活、低成本地实现大规模海上交通监测。但实际海事环境中存在异构传感模态、恶劣成像条件以及无人机运动导致的空间错位等挑战，实现稳健态势感知仍很困难。

Method: 提出机载多模态图学习驱动的AMGT框架。首先通过多参数透视变换模型对齐并融合可见光与红外图像；其次用插值和线性预测对AIS消息进行时间同步；接着开发混合模态MAir-YOLOv8-OBB检测器和MAir-SORT跟踪算法；最后设计图学习驱动的关联网络建模船舶间及船舶-无人机间的时空依赖，并引入运动感知坐标统一（MCU）策略以减小无人机运动引起的非线性错位。

Result: 在MAP-M2UAV50数据集上，AMGT取得了较高的准确率和效率，验证了其在船舶监控、交通态势感知与智能海事运输服务等无人机辅助海事物联网应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: AMGT有效应对异构模态、恶劣成像和空间错位等挑战，为无人机辅助海事物联网提供了有前景的解决方案。数据集与代码将在 https://github.com/MAPGroup/AMGT 公开。

Abstract: The unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based maritime surveillance is becoming an important part of maritime Internet of Things (IoT) and intelligent transportation systems. It provides a flexible and cost-effective way to large-scale maritime traffic monitoring. However, achieving robust situational awareness remains challenging in practical maritime environments. The main challenges arise from heterogeneous sensing modalities, adverse imaging conditions, and spatial misalignment caused by UAV motion. To address these problems, this paper proposes an airborne multi-modal graph learning-driven framework, termed AMGT, for vessel identification and tracking in UAV-assisted maritime IoT. Specifically, visible and infrared images are first aligned and fused through a multi-parameter perspective transformation model. This improves visual perception under low visibility and complex illumination conditions. Automatic identification system (AIS) messages are then temporally synchronized using interpolation and linear prediction. To detect small and oriented vessels, we develop a mixed-modality MAir-YOLOv8-OBB detector, followed by the MAir-SORT algorithm for stable vessel tracking. We further design a graph learning-driven association network to model temporal dynamics and spatial dependencies among vessels and between vessels and UAVs. A motion-aware coordinate unification (MCU) strategy is also introduced to reduce nonlinear misalignment caused by UAV motion. Experiments on the MAP-M2UAV50 dataset show that AMGT achieves high accuracy and efficiency. The results demonstrate its potential for UAV-assisted maritime IoT applications, including vessel monitoring, traffic situational awareness, and intelligent maritime transportation services. The dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/MAPGroup/AMGT.

</details>


### [62] [Automated Detection and Encoding of Cartographic Signs in Historical Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13224)
*Remi Petitpierre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段框架用于历史地图符号检测与可解释嵌入生成，并发布基准数据集CaSiDD，为地图符号的可扩展证据提取奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 档案地图中的符号与图标是历史地理研究的重要证据来源，但需要自动化手段来检测和表征这些制图符号，以支持制图文化与地理历史研究中的大规模比较分析。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：（1）使用YOLOv10-X在历史地图中检测制图符号；（2）设计可解释的符号嵌入，系统性地最大化表达能力、抑制数字化或背景材料等混杂因素，最终采用基于改进RADIOv4骨干网络的方法编码符号的形式与语义。同时构建了跨多类别与多制图风格的开放基准数据集CaSiDD。

Result: YOLOv10-X验证了自动化符号检测在历史地图上的实用性；基于改进RADIOv4的嵌入方法能有效编码符号形式与语义，并生成可解释的表示。

Conclusion: 该工作为制图文化与地理历史研究中地图符号比较研究提供了可扩展的证据提取基础。

Abstract: Symbols and icons in archival maps represent an expansive source of evidence for historical geography. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework for (1) detecting cartographic signs in historical maps and (2) generating interpretable sign embeddings that capture both semantics and stylistic variations. We also introduce CaSiDD, an open benchmark dataset spanning multiple sign categories and diverse cartographic styles, designed for generic sign detection. Using YOLOv10-X, we demonstrate the practicality of automated sign detection on historical maps. We then present strategies for creating contrasted, informative embeddings of cartographic signs. We approach embedding design systematically, maximizing expressiveness while suppressing digitization or background material confounders. Our final method, based on an adapted RADIOv4 backbone, effectively encodes both sign form and semantics, while producing interpretable representations. This work lays a foundation for scalable evidence extraction for the comparative study of map signs within cartographic culture and geohistorical research.

</details>


### [63] [FPD-Drive: Future Policy Distillation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13232)
*Li-Heng Chen,Qifeng Chen,Chengwei Wu,Yingjie Zhang,Sheng Yang,Hongbo Fu,Shaoqing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FPD-Drive，通过未来特权知识蒸馏实现多目标监督与安全候选选择，在 NAVSIM 和 HUGSIM 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型通常只基于真实驾驶日志中实际执行的轨迹训练，缺乏对替代有效行为的监督。多目标监督能缓解该问题，但更广的轨迹覆盖使安全候选选择更具挑战。

Method: 提出 FPD-Drive 端到端规划器，将特权未来知识蒸馏到视觉驾驶策略中。包括未来特权多目标蒸馏（从可访问记录未来观测的教师模型迁移多样且高质量的轨迹目标）以及安全感知分数校正（修正相近安全与不安全候选间的排序错误，提升被低估的安全候选分数）。部署时学生模型无需未来观测或教师推理。

Result: 基础模型在 NAVSIM v1 navtest 上达 94.9 PDMS，在 NAVSIM v2 navhard 上达 55.9 EPDMS；扩展版 FPD-Drive-Pro 分别提升至 95.5 PDMS 和 58.1 EPDMS，在两个基准上均取得对比方法中的 SOTA。在 HUGSIM 的零样本闭环评估中取得 HD-Score 45.1、RC 55.3。

Conclusion: FPD-Drive 通过未来特权蒸馏与安全感知分数校正，有效提升了多目标监督与安全选择能力，并在多个基准上验证了其有效性与部署友好性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are typically trained on real-world driving logs, which record only the trajectory actually executed in each scene. This provides limited supervision for learning alternative valid behaviors. Multi-target supervision can mitigate this limitation, but broader trajectory coverage makes safe candidate selection more challenging. We propose FPD-Drive, an end-to-end planner that distills privileged future knowledge into a visual driving policy to improve both multi-trajectory supervision and safety selection. Specifically, we introduce Future-Privileged Multi-Target Distillation to transfer diverse, high-quality trajectory targets from a teacher with access to recorded future observations to the student. These targets extend supervision beyond the single recorded human trajectory. For candidate selection, we further introduce Safety-Aware Score Correction to correct ranking errors between nearby safe and unsafe candidates and raise underestimated safety scores of safe candidates. At deployment, the student requires neither future observations nor teacher inference. Our base model achieves 94.9 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest and 55.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navhard. The scaled FPD-Drive-Pro improves these scores to 95.5 PDMS and 58.1 EPDMS, respectively, achieving SOTA performance among the compared methods on both benchmarks. Zero-shot closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM further yields an HD-Score of 45.1 and RC of 55.3.

</details>


### [64] [Jag: Direct Box Prediction for Efficient Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13239)
*Xiuyuan Zhu,Ke Lu,Hao Wu,Zijin Du,Dongming Zhang,Jian Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Jag 将紧凑预训练多模态模型改造为直接连续框预测器：轻量回归头读取最后一个有效输入 token 状态，一次前向同时输出四坐标，几何监督联合适配语言骨干、视觉合并器与回归头，在提升定位精度的同时降低延迟与显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归多模态模型通常把目标框表示为坐标序列，顺序解码会为定位额外增加计算开销，且需要文本解析。作者希望绕开坐标生成，直接用几何表示实现直接预测，使定位损失能塑造多模态表征与坐标映射。

Method: 基于紧凑预训练多模态模型构建 Jag：在模型末端加入轻量回归头，读取由图像和指代表达条件化的最后一个有效输入 token 状态，一次性预测四个框坐标；训练时使用几何监督联合适配语言骨干、视觉合并器与回归头；推理时保留图文处理流程，一次前向直接返回框，无需坐标生成或文本解析。

Result: 在各基准上，Jag 在所观察的目标尺寸与表达分组上均稳定优于其基座模型；组件消融显示联合适配表征与回归头显著优于仅训练回归头；与现有专用定位模型相比，在单请求延迟和 GPU 显存占用更低的情况下取得有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 针对几何答案定制的监督方式，可将紧凑预训练多模态模型特化为兼具高定位精度与高效直接预测能力的模型。

Abstract: Visual grounding combines image--language understanding with geometric prediction, yet autoregressive multimodal models often express a box as a coordinate sequence. Sequential decoding adds computation to the multimodal processing needed for localization. Adapting these representations to box geometry offers a route to direct prediction. We present Jag, which adapts a compact pretrained multimodal model for direct continuous box prediction. A lightweight regression head reads the last valid input-token state, conditioned on the image and referring expression, and predicts all four box coordinates together. Geometric supervision jointly adapts the language backbone, visual merger, and regression head, allowing localization losses to shape both the multimodal representations and the coordinate mapping. Inference retains image--language processing and returns the box in one forward pass, without coordinate generation or text parsing. Across benchmarks, Jag consistently improves grounding accuracy over its base model across the observed target sizes and expression groups. Component ablation show that jointly adapting the representations and head substantially improves accuracy over training the head alone. Against existing specialized grounding models, it achieves competitive accuracy with lower single-request latency and GPU memory use. These results show how supervision tailored to the geometric answer can specialize a compact pretrained multimodal model for accurate grounding and efficient direct prediction. Project page: https://xyzzzh.github.io/Jag/

</details>


### [65] [Deep Learning Approach for RealTime Contraction Detection in Laboring Dairy Cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13242)
*Sebastian Puchala,Dominik Gronkiewicz,Marcin Gołębiewski,Grzegorz Grodkowski,Grzegorz Gwardys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于计算机视觉的奶牛分娩宫缩自动识别方法，利用CNN和ViT从视频帧中提取特征，识别准确率超过95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畜牧生产领域正快速发展动物行为与健康的自动监测技术；实现奶牛分娩宫缩的自动识别，可提升乳业生产效率与动物福利，并在难产时支持及时干预。

Method: 采集100头奶牛分娩视频，构建包含8500个宫缩动作和约25000个非宫缩序列的数据集；结合图像处理技术与深度学习算法（卷积神经网络CNN和视觉Transformer ViT），从视频帧中提取相关特征以检测并分类宫缩。

Result: 宫缩检测准确率超过95%；系统演示视频发布于指定URL。

Conclusion: 该自动化系统有望通过实时监测与难产及时干预，提升乳业生产效率与动物福利。

Abstract: Currently, the animal production sector is undergoing rapid development of techniques that allow automatic monitoring of animal behaviour and health. This paper presents a computer vision-based approach for the automatic recognition of labor contractions in cows. During the course of the study, videos depicting 100 cow births were collected. From the obtained videos, 8500 cow contraction actions and about 25 000 non-contraction sequences were extracted which constituted the dataset. Leveraging image processing techniques and deep learning algorithms like Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs), our methodology extracts relevant features from video frames to detect and classify contractions. Our study demonstrates a high accuracy rate of over 95% in detecting labor contractions. This automated system has the potential to enhance productivity and animal welfare in the dairy industry by providing real-time monitoring and facilitating timely intervention during difficult calvings. The video demonstration of the system can be found at the following URL: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8fTSG5D7dZ0

</details>


### [66] [Vision Transformers Learn Word Meaning from Images Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13252)
*Anders Gjølbye,Sebastian Ray Mason,Stella Frank,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 纯视觉自监督训练的 DINOv3 模型能从渲染文字的像素中习得词汇语义结构，无需任何语言监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仅用图像自监督预训练（无配对字幕、类别标签或语言监督）的视觉模型，是否能从像素中学习词义，以及词汇语义是否会在纯视觉模型中自发涌现。

Method: 以两个最大的 DINOv3 视觉 Transformer 为对象，通过"词图像与匹配对照图像的嵌入差"提取词表示；在控制拼写相似性的条件下，检验语义相关词在嵌入空间中是否互为近邻，并考察国名-首都向量偏移、词表示与对应视觉物体类别表示的对齐；再与文本、语音编码器比较表征共享结构，并进行对比对齐微调实验。

Result: 同行业品牌名、同语义域名词在嵌入空间中聚集；国名-首都对呈现与文本词向量类似的稳定向量偏移；词表示与所指视觉物体类别表示对齐。DINOv3-H 和 DINOv3-7B 是首批展现出显著词汇语义结构的自监督视觉模型；与文本或语音编码器对比显示相同词的表征存在共享结构；对比对齐可增强 DINOv3-H 的词级语义结构，而其他微调视觉模型基本不变。

Conclusion: 仅图像训练不仅能让视觉模型学会词"长什么样"，还能学会词义之间的关系，说明纯视觉自监督可诱导出词汇层面的语义组织。

Abstract: Can a vision model learn what words mean from pixels alone? We show that the two largest DINOv3 vision transformers organize words rendered in images by word meaning, despite being pretrained using self-supervision on images only, without paired captions, class labels, or other language supervision. We derive word representations from embedding differences between word images and matched controls, then test whether semantically related words are neighbors within each model's embedding space, while controlling for spelling similarity. We find that brand names in the same industry group together, as do nouns from the same semantic domain. Country-capital pairs exhibit consistent vector offsets, similarly to word embeddings trained on text. These word representations also align with the representations of the visual object categories they name. DINOv3-H and DINOv3-7B are, to our knowledge, the first self-supervised vision models to show substantial semantic structure for rendered words. Comparisons with text and speech encoders reveal shared structure among representations of the same words. Contrastive alignment with either encoder further strengthened DINOv3-H's word-level semantic structure, while leaving the other finetuned vision models largely unchanged. Image-only training can therefore teach vision models not just what words look like, but also how their meanings relate.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond a Single Crop: Contextualized Embeddings for Player Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13259)
*Ge Luo,Changhuo Yang,Weisi Fan,Rui Yang,Shaokai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ContextReID 框架，利用同帧共现球员与跨帧观测的上下文信息，通过视觉语言模型生成上下文感知嵌入，提升广播体育视频中的球员再识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播体育视频中同队球员外观高度相似，仅依赖单张裁剪图像的嵌入方法忽略了帧内共现球员与跨帧观测等关键上下文信息，难以区分视觉上相似的身份。

Method: 构建 ContextReID 上下文嵌入框架，将同一帧中其他球员的裁剪图像与不同帧的观测一并输入视觉语言模型，联合建模空间共现与跨帧信息；采用上下文学习与检索相结合的联合目标函数，在保持判别性嵌入的同时捕获跨帧身份关系。

Result: 在三个多目标跟踪基准上的实验表明，ContextReID 在 Rank-1 与 mAP 两项指标上均优于传统的单裁剪嵌入方法，提升了球员检索性能。

Conclusion: 空间与时间上下文对广播体育视频中的可靠球员识别具有重要价值，上下文感知嵌入是应对同队外观相似问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Person re-identification is typically addressed by learning identity-discriminative embeddings from individual person crops. However, player re-identification in broadcast sports videos presents a unique challenge: players from the same team often share similar appearances, while video sequences provide repeated observations of the same players under different viewpoints and surrounding contexts. Existing crop-based approaches largely ignore this contextual information, which can be critical for resolving visually similar identities.
  Here, we propose ContextReID, a contextualized embedding framework that represents a player by jointly considering other players in the same frame and observations from different frames of the broadcast sequence. By incorporating both co-occurring players and cross-frame observations, the model learns identity features relative to the surrounding visual context rather than relying only on isolated appearance cues. We implement this framework with a vision-language model that takes sets of player crops as input and generates context-aware embeddings for individual players. A combined contextual learning and retrieval objective encourages the model to capture cross-frame identity relationships while maintaining discriminative embeddings for player matching.
  Experiments across three multi-object tracking benchmarks show that ContextReID improves player retrieval over conventional single-crop embedding approaches in both Rank-1 and mAP. These results highlight the utility of spatial and temporal context for reliable player identification in broadcast sports videos.

</details>


### [68] [SheafPrune: Sheaf-Guided Graph Propagation for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13263)
*Xiaoyang Ma,Chi Wang,Hong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SheafPrune 用类似层论几何的稀疏文图图结构传播相关性并做节点度数修正，在冻结 LLM 前一次性保留 top-k 视觉 token，实现更好的精度—成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型处理完整视觉 token 序列导致预填充开销大，即使许多 token 与指令无关；现有 token 级显著性方法难以捕捉 token 间依赖，而基于图的选取又依赖关系如何决定重要性传播与排序。

Method: 研究带支撑约束、带节点度数修正的传播；受层论几何启发，SheafPrune 使用模态特定映射与稀疏文本—视觉图传递相关性，再在独立视觉图上传播分数并进行度数修正，在冻结语言模型前一次性保留硬 top-k 子集。选择器约 4.5M 参数，仅用语言建模目标端到端训练（LLaVA-NeXT-8B 冻结骨干），并通过显式保留率条件使一个 checkpoint 适配所有预算。

Result: 在四个骨干、十个基准、七种剪枝率、统一协议重实现所有基线下，SheafPrune 在 28 个骨干—预算设置中的 22 个取得最佳保留率，且在中高剪枝区间优势单调增大；在 LLaVA-NeXT 上保留 10% 视觉 token 获得 5.15× 净预填充加速与 84.0% 端到端 FLOPs 降低。

Conclusion: 结合组件消融，结果支持图约束的重要性传播与度数修正作为视觉 token 选择设计，在所评估骨干与分词方案上改善精度保留与推理成本的权衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models process the complete visual token sequence, increasing prefill cost even when many tokens are irrelevant to the instruction. Token-wise saliency captures limited inter-token dependence; graph-based selection instead depends on how relations govern importance propagation and ranking. We study support-constrained propagation with node-degree correction. Inspired by sheaf geometry, SheafPrune uses modality-specific maps and a sparse text--visual graph to transfer relevance, then propagates scores on a separate visual graph and corrects them for degree. It retains a hard top-$k$ subset once before the frozen language model. The selector has about $4.5$M parameters and is trained end-to-end through the frozen LLaVA-NeXT-8B backbone using only the language-modeling objective. Explicit retention-ratio conditioning lets one checkpoint serve every budget. Across four backbones, ten benchmarks and seven pruning ratios, with all baselines re-implemented under one protocol, SheafPrune achieves the best retention at $22$ of $28$ backbone--budget settings, with monotonically growing margins in the moderate-to-aggressive regime. On LLaVA-NeXT, retaining $10\%$ of visual tokens yields a $5.15\times$ net prefill speedup and an $84.0\%$ end-to-end FLOPs reduction. Together with component ablations, these results support graph-constrained importance propagation and degree correction as a design for visual token selection across the evaluated backbones and tokenization schemes, improving the trade-off between accuracy retention and inference cost. code:https://github.com/Bob0817912/SheafPrune_ICLR_2027

</details>


### [69] [CV-OPD: Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13264)
*Haowei Wang,Hao Lin,Qi Zhu,Jiayi Ji,Deyi Ji,Liujuan Cao,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现on-policy distillation (OPD)在VLM后训练中存在捷径问题：学生模型即使相关视觉区域被遮挡或加噪仍能答对，说明其未真正依赖视觉证据；为此提出CV-OPD，通过对比正/负教师分布并加margin对比约束，缓解捷径并提升视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到OPD蒸馏后的VLM在视觉区域被遮挡或替换为噪声时仍能准确作答，说明OPD借助答案作为提示，未能教会学生真正寻找并依赖视觉证据。理论分析认为，这一失败源于蒸馏过程中缺乏视觉约束所导致的捷径。因此需要一种方法来纠正学生对视觉区域的使用方式。

Method: 提出Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD)。它比较在同一学生前缀条件下，由正样本裁剪与负样本裁剪诱导出的教师分布，并在优化中加入margin-based对比约束，同时用token级和样本级不一致性进行门控调制。具体包括：引入与答案无关的负教师，设计Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL)，通过对比正负教师来约束学生特征分布，纠正学生对视觉区域的利用；以及引入Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG)，利用token级和样本级差异选择视觉相关token进行优化，引导学生注意力转向视觉内容。

Result: 在广泛的细粒度视觉基准上的大量实验表明，CV-OPD显著缓解了捷径问题，并持续提升了现有VLM的视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: CV-OPD通过对比正负教师分布并配合差异感知门控，有效解决了OPD中因缺乏视觉约束而产生的捷径问题，使VLM后训练更依赖真实视觉证据，在多个细粒度视觉基准上稳定提升视觉推理性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reveals versatility and convenience during VLM post-training, providing dense supervision for student from teacher. However, we observed an anomaly that the distilled VLM could still answer the questions accurately even when the related visual regions were masked or replaced with noise.
