It achieves the best CLIP and physical controllability scores on both common and uncommon materials, with qualitative comparisons, component-level ablations, and a user study further supporting these improvements. Our project page is available at https://masterwanna.github.io/PhysForge.html .

</details>


### [103] [X-AVDD: Cross-Attentive Audio-Visual Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13687)
*Saumyaranjan Mohanty,Aravind Reddy,Konda Reddy Mopuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 X-AVDD，一种基于交叉注意力的跨模态音视频数据集蒸馏方法，显著降低显存开销并提升合成数据的跨模态对齐与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频数据集蒸馏方法（如 DM、AVDD）存在两大局限：一是内存占用高，难以扩展到复杂架构和高 SPC 设置；二是难以保持强跨模态语义对应关系。

Method: X-AVDD 采用交叉注意力机制，在蒸馏合成过程中显式捕捉跨模态（音频-视觉）关系；通过将跨模态对齐从联合优化中解耦，实现更高的内存效率。

Result: 端到端合成成本降低约 8 倍 GPU 小时；在 VGGSound 上，SPC=10 时 top-1 准确率由 8.2% 提升至 24.0%，SPC=20 时由 9.8% 提升至 26.5%；在 AVE 数据集 SPC=50 下，ViT 目标模型的跨架构泛化由 25.5% 提升至 35.3%；可扩展至 AudioCLIP 等复杂架构，仅用 5.65% 数据达到 62.3% 准确率（全数据集为 72.4%），训练时间比全数据集少 9 倍。

Conclusion: X-AVDD 在显著降低内存与训练成本的同时，大幅超越现有音视频蒸馏方法，并具备良好的跨架构泛化与复杂模型可扩展性。

Abstract: Audio-visual dataset distillation aims to synthesize a small dataset from a large paired audio-visual dataset, while nearly preserving the training utility for neural networks. Existing approaches such as DM and AVDD face two critical limitations: 1) they are memory-intensive, making them difficult to scale to complex architectures and higher samples-per-class (SPC) settings, and 2) they often fail to preserve strong cross-modal semantic correspondence. To tackle these issues, we propose X-AVDD, a cross-attention-based distillation method that explicitly captures inter-modal relationships during synthesis. By decoupling cross-modal alignment from joint optimization, X-AVDD is substantially more memory-efficient, reducing end-to-end synthesis cost by $\sim 8\times$ GPU-hours, while producing highly aligned synthetic data. Empirically, X-AVDD significantly outperforms other audio-visual distillation methods. On VGGSound, an audio-visual dataset of short YouTube clips, X-AVDD improves top-1 test accuracy from $8.2\%\rightarrow24.0\%$ at SPC=$10$ and $9.8\%\rightarrow26.5\%$ at SPC=$20$. Furthermore, X-AVDD improves cross-architecture generalization (e.g., $25.5\%\rightarrow35.3\%$ for a ViT target on AVE dataset at SPC $=50$) and successfully scales to complex architectures such as AudioCLIP, achieving $62.3\%$ accuracy using only $5.65\%$ of the full dataset, against the full dataset accuracy of $72.4\%$ while completing in $9\times$ lower training time compared to the full dataset.

</details>


### [104] [Improving Semantic Coverage in AI Test & Evaluation through Generative Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13697)
*Connor Hashemi,Bharadwaj Ravichandran,Connor Anderson,Shitij Govil,Brandon RichardWebster,Brian Hu,Keith Fieldhouse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段生成式AI流水线，在无需语义真值标签的情况下检测并填补AI模型测试评估中的语义覆盖缺口，仅用于增强测试集，不重训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI模型的测试与评估（T&E）中，评估者通常只能获得留出测试集，不了解训练数据或其语义构成。若关键输入条件（如时段、天气、场景位置）在测试集中欠代表，会因语义覆盖缺口导致评估结论产生误导。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1）用视觉语言模型对语义因素做零样本二值标注，并通过嵌入空间聚类识别对下游模型行为影响最大的语义；2）用基于扩散的图生图模型将已覆盖图像转换到欠代表的语义域，在保留场景内容的同时增强高层外观；3）对增强图像的嵌入做统计分析，验证合成图像是否提升数据集覆盖度，验证通过后才纳入。合成数据仅用于丰富测试集，不进行模型重训练。

Result: 在图像分类和目标检测任务上、针对白天/黑夜语义缺口验证整个流水线，表明增强后的测试集能更好地区分是否接触过缺失语义的模型，其T&E结论更接近理想全覆盖数据集的结果。

Conclusion: 该流水线可在无真值语义标签条件下检测并填补测试集语义覆盖缺口，使T&E结论更贴近理想覆盖情形，且不改变被评估模型本身。代码已开源。

Abstract: In test and evaluation (T&E) of AI models, evaluators typically have access only to a held-out test dataset and no knowledge of the training data or its semantic composition. If critical input conditions---such as time-of-day, weather, or scene location---are underrepresented in this test dataset, T&E results can be misleadingly due to this semantic coverage gap. We propose a three-stage generative AI pipeline to detect and fill such semantic gaps without requiring ground-truth labels of these semantics. First, vision-language models (VLMs) perform zero-shot binary labeling of semantic factors, and an embedding-space clustering step identifies which semantics most affect downstream model behavior. Second, diffusion-based image-to-image models transform existing covered images into the underrepresented semantic domain, preserving scene content while augmenting high-level appearance. Third, statistical analysis of the augmented image embeddings verifies that the synthetic imagery improves dataset coverage before it is incorporated. Crucially, the synthetic data is used only to enrich the test set and no model retraining is performed for T&E. We validate the full pipeline on both image classification and object detection under day/night semantic gaps, showing that the augmented test datasets better distinguish models with and without exposure to the missing semantic, thereby yielding T&E conclusions that more closely match those of an ideal, fully covered dataset. Code available at https://github.com/Hashe037/DEDUCE

</details>


### [105] [GLIMPSE: Global-Local Indexing with Multi-Precision Spectral Encoding for Explorable Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13706)
*Zhufeng Xu,Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Yikang Ding,Xiaoqiang Liu,Guoxin Zhang,Hongbo Fu,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLIMPSE提出一种全局-局部记忆接口，通过分带多精度坐标与几何条件谱衰减，降低不准确投影地址的敏感度，提升相机控制视频世界模型在场景回访时的几何与光度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机控制的视频世界模型在相机回到曾见场景时需保持一致性。将存储特征渲染到目标帧的记忆方法会把深度与位姿误差放大为鬼影或重复结构；位置传输虽通过迁移历史patch的地址而非内容来避免重采样，却对不准确的投影仍赋予完整的位置精度，导致误差敏感。

Method: 提出GLIMPSE，一种全局-局部记忆接口：采用分带多精度坐标（band-wise multi-precision coordinates）与几何条件谱衰减（geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation）来降低对不准确投影地址的敏感度；局部patch记忆提供视角相关外观，循环全局记忆提供场景上下文；并通过足迹引导检索（footprint-guided retrieval）在固定局部读取预算下选取历史patch。

Result: 在匹配的检索与读取预算下，GLIMPSE在几何对应与光度一致性上优于确定性的位置传输方法。

Conclusion: 通过多精度坐标与谱衰减抑制不准确投影地址的影响，并结合局部外观与全局上下文记忆，GLIMPSE有效提升相机控制视频世界模型在场景回访时的时空一致性。

Abstract: A camera-controlled video world model must keep a scene consistent when the camera returns to it. Memories that render stored features into the target frame can turn depth and pose errors into ghosting or duplicated structure. Positional transport avoids that resampling by relocating a historical patch's address rather than its content, but assigns full positional precision even to inaccurate projections. We present GLIMPSE, a global-local memory interface with band-wise multi-precision coordinates and geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation to reduce sensitivity to inaccurate projected addresses. Local patch memory supplies view-specific appearance, while recurrent global memory provides scene context. Footprint-guided retrieval selects historical patches under a fixed local read budget. Under matched retrieval and read budgets, GLIMPSE improves geometric correspondence and photometric consistency over deterministic positional transport.

</details>


### [106] [State-Isolated Vision Mamba via Update-Tendency Routing for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13720)
*Yan Luo,Laibin Chang,Zefan Feng,Yi'ang Chen,Kui Jiang,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"状态争用"问题，并基于"状态隔离"原则构建IsoMamba，通过聚类-路由-隔离范式实现水下图像增强的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Mamba研究主要改进token序列化、扫描策略或专家路由，但忽视了状态层面的关键问题：如何为空间异质的视觉恢复任务组织循环状态。作者发现，让具有异质更新行为的token递归修改共享隐藏状态会导致持续的跨内容干扰，即"状态争用"。

Method: 提出状态隔离原则，让具有兼容状态更新倾向的token在独立的循环状态流中演化。据此设计IsoMamba架构：利用Δ引导构建模型内在的更新表示，通过无标签聚类发现更新兼容的token组，并按照"聚类-路由-隔离"范式将其路由到隔离的Mamba专家。此外，针对水下图像波长相关的重建难度，开发了动态通道重加权目标。

Result: 大量实验表明，IsoMamba在多个水下图像增强基准上持续达到SOTA性能，同时提供了更清晰的隐藏状态响应。

Conclusion: 有效的循环建模应按token兼容性而非token顺序来组织状态演化，状态隔离的循环建模在水下图像增强任务中被验证有效。

Abstract: Vision Mamba has recently emerged as an efficient alternative to convolutional and Transformer-based architectures by modeling long-range spatial dependencies through recurrent state evolution. Existing studies primarily improve token serialization, scanning strategies, or expert routing, yet largely overlook a state level question question: how should recurrent states be organized for spatially heterogeneous visual restoration? In this paper, we show that forcing tokens with heterogeneous update behaviors to recurrently modify a shared hidden state leads to persistent cross-content interference, which we term \textbf{State Contention}. We further argue that effective recurrent modeling should organize state evolution according to token compatibility rather than token ordering. Based on this observation, we introduce \textbf{State Isolation}, a new recurrent state organization principle for Vision Mamba,tokens with compatible state-update tendencies evolve within independent recurrent state streams. To instantiate this principle, we propose \textbf{IsoMamba}, a $Δ$-guided architecture that constructs model-intrinsic update representations, discovers update-compatible token groups through label-free clustering, and routes them to isolated Mamba experts following a \textbf{cluster--route--isolate} paradigm. In addition, we develop a dynamic channel-wise reweighting objective to compensate for wavelength-dependent reconstruction difficulty in underwater images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IsoMamba consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks while providing cleaner hidden state responses, validating the effectiveness of state isolated recurrent modeling.

</details>


### [107] [LAPT-Scale: Scaling Embodied Policies with Latent Action Pre-Training on Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13732)
*Zhiyuan Xiang,Peidong Liu,Mingyang Li,Jiawei Li,Wenhow Li,Wenbo Ding,Xiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LAPT-Scale 可扩展潜在动作预训练范式，利用 23.3K 小时自我中心人类视频学习连续潜在动作来监督 VLA 策略，将真实机器人成功率从 63.3% 提升至 85.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略的可扩展性受限于具身交互数据的高昂采集成本。大规模第一人称人类视频是丰富替代来源，但缺少机器人动作标签；现有动作标注方法在真实世界视觉复杂度与多样化操作动力学下往往噪声较大。

Method: LAPT-Scale 包含两部分：LAPT-Scale-LAM 通过解释人类视频中帧间视觉转移来学习连续潜在动作，作为人类衍生的监督信号；LAPT-Scale-Policy 通过人类视频潜在动作预训练、目标机器人具身对齐和任务特定后训练来训练 VLA 策略。与先前向量量化的 LAM 不同，该方法采用连续潜在空间以保留细粒度运动与交互动力学，并融合感知、语义与光流对齐信号，超越像素重建水平提升转移级监督。模型在 23.3K 小时自我中心视频上训练。

Result: 当标注人类视频规模从 1K 增至 5K 小时，性能展现一致的扩展增益，真实机器人成功率从 63.3% 提升至 85.0%；所学策略还能泛化到未见过的视觉条件。

Conclusion: LAPT-Scale 表明大规模人类视频的连续潜在动作监督可有效预训练机器人策略，在降低数据采集成本的同时带来可扩展的性能提升与视觉泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling generalist robotic policies is limited by the high cost of collecting embodied interaction data. Large-scale egocentric human videos offer a rich alternative, but they lack robot action labels, and existing action annotation methods are often noisy under real-world visual complexity and diverse manipulation dynamics. We introduce LAPT-Scale, a scalable latent action pre-training paradigm with two components: LAPT-Scale-LAM learns continuous latent actions by explaining frame-to-frame visual transitions in human videos and uses them as human-derived supervision, while LAPT-Scale-Policy trains a VLA policy through human-video latent-action pre-training, target-robot embodiment alignment, and task-specific post-training. Unlike prior vector-quantized LAMs, LAPT-Scale-LAM uses a continuous latent space to preserve fine-grained motion and interaction dynamics. We further combine perceptual, semantic, and optical-flow alignment signals to improve transition-level supervision beyond pixel reconstruction. Trained on 23.3K hours of egocentric video, LAPT-Scale-LAM provides implicit action supervision for policy pre-training, yielding consistent scaling gains and improving real-robot success from 63.3% to 85.0% as annotated human video scales from 1K to 5K hours. The learned policy also generalizes to unseen visual conditions.

</details>


### [108] [KVeil: First-Place Solution to ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13733)
*Boheng Li,Yueming Xing,Yiming Li,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge不可学习视频挑战赛两个阶段的第一名方案，均基于LETHE，通过参考容量崩溃、运动引导EOT及两层区域保护框架，在有限不可感知扰动下有效防止人脸视频被未授权定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视频生成快速发展，少量人脸视频即可被用来复现个人身份，引发严重的身份隐私担忧。现有方法需在有限且不可感知的扰动预算下，保护人脸视频免遭未授权定制，且需应对未知推理条件、基于微调的定制以及未见生成模型等多重威胁。

Method: 方案基于其近期工作LETHE。第一阶段提出参考容量崩溃（Reference Capacity Collapse），由一对互补损失构成，削弱参考条件所携带的信息同时保持后续视频表示对其的依赖，并引入运动引导EOT机制以提升对未知推理条件的鲁棒性。第二阶段针对基于微调的定制与未见生成模型，设计两层框架，分别保护人脸与背景区域：前者基于合成目标身份构建结构对齐扰动，后者强化第一阶段的白盒破坏效果，从而在单一扰动中同时应对两类威胁。

Result: 在白盒基于参考的定制场景下实现了近乎完美的保护效果，并在其他定制范式、多种模型以及时序攻击下依然保持有效。

Conclusion: 该方案为保护人脸视频免遭未授权定制提供了有效解决思路，两个阶段方案均建立在LETHE工作之上，作者希望其方案能启发未来相关研究，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid development of personalized video generation has raised serious concerns over identity privacy, as a few facial videos of a person now suffice to reproduce their identity in synthetic videos. This report presents the first-place solutions to both phases of the ECCV 2026 LifeGenIP Challenge on Unlearnable Videos, which aims to protect facial videos from unauthorized customization under a limited and imperceptible perturbation budget. For Phase 1, we introduce Reference Capacity Collapse, a pair of complementary losses that degrade the information carried by the reference condition while keeping subsequent video representations dependent on it, together with a Motion-Guided EOT mechanism that improves robustness across unknown inference conditions. Phase 2 further considers tuning-based customization and unseen generative models. To address both threats within a single perturbation, we design a two-layer framework that protects the facial and background regions separately, constructing structure-aligned perturbations from a synthetic target identity in the former and strengthening the white-box disruption of Phase 1 in the latter. Our method achieves nearly perfect protection under white-box reference-based customization, and remains effective under other customization paradigms, various models, and temporal attacks. Both solutions are built upon our recent work LETHE. We hope our solutions can inspire future work on protecting facial videos against unauthorized customization. Our code is available at https://github.com/AntigoneRandy/KVeil.

</details>


### [109] [Gaussian Splatting with Reflections Guided by What is Seen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13757)
*Yiming Liang,Tianyu Xiao,Hiroshi Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于2D Gaussian Splatting的框架，利用输入图像中可直接观测的环境信息，结合混合反射外观表示与视角相关镜面建模，在近场反射场景中实现更优的渲染质量与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如NeRF和3DGS在常见场景表现优异，但在捕捉视角相关镜面效果（尤其是近场反射）时存在不足。环境贴图只能表示无穷远处的光照，环境高斯仅依赖反射的间接监督，均难以准确建模此类效果。

Method: 基于2D Gaussian Splatting（2DGS）构建框架，直接利用输入图像中可观测的环境信息，并集成混合反射外观表示与视角相关的镜面建模。

Result: 在公开基准以及新构建的近场反射数据集上进行实验，结果表明该方法在渲染质量和几何精度上均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 通过直接利用可观测环境信息并结合混合反射与视角相关镜面建模，可有效提升近场反射等复杂镜面场景的新视角合成质量与几何准确性。

Abstract: While novel view synthesis methods like Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have shown impressive performance on common scenes, they often struggle with capturing view-dependent specular effects, particularly near-field reflections. Existing methods utilize environment maps, which only represent lights from infinity, or environment Gaussians, which rely solely on indirect supervision from reflections, are insufficient for accurately modeling such effects. Here, we propose a framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) that leverages directly observable environment information in input images. Our approach also integrates a hybrid reflection appearance representation and view-dependent specular modeling. Experiments on public benchmarks and a newly curated near-field reflection dataset demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [110] [Neurons++: A Unified Visual-Stream Framework for Image and Video Reconstruction from fMRI with Neural-to-Visual Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13763)
*Haonan Wang,Yuchen Wang,Jingyu Lu,Qixiang Zhang,Lehan Wang,Xuanqi Huang,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Neurons++ 是一个视觉流 fMRI 重建框架，可同时用于静态图像与动态视频重建，采用类皮层串行解码设计，在视频重建上达到 SOTA，并具备神经扰动可控性与 ROI 引导的语义调制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 fMRI 解码视觉体验对理解人类感知和开发脑机接口很重要。现有生成式解码虽能重建看似合理的图像和视频，但视觉上的合理性并不能保证输出忠实于大脑活动，因此需要更忠实于脑信号的解码方法。

Method: 提出 Neurons++，相较会议版 Neurons，从仅视频重建扩展到图像与视频的视觉流重建，并用类皮层的串行解码替代并行任务条件化。模型先用图像-fMRI 预训练获得稳定的空间-语义初始化，再分别针对图像和视频重建进行被试特定的微调；串行设计将 fMRI 表征组织为显式的空间、语义、语言层和时间线索。

Result: 在标准基准上，Neurons++ 实现了 SOTA 的视频重建和具有竞争力的图像重建。神经-视觉分析显示其神经扰动下具有定性方向可控性，fMRI 编码器读出对刺激相关的皮层特征敏感，并支持 ROI 引导的语义调制。

Conclusion: Neurons++ 通过串行类皮层解码与分阶段训练，将 fMRI 视觉重建统一扩展到图像和视频，兼顾重建保真度与对脑活动的忠实性，为脑机接口与感知研究提供可用框架，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from fMRI is important for understanding human perception and developing brain-computer interfaces. Recent generative decoding methods can reconstruct plausible images and videos, but visual plausibility alone does not ensure that the output is faithfully grounded in brain activity. In this work, we propose Neurons++, a visual-stream fMRI reconstruction framework for both static image and dynamic video reconstruction. Compared with our conference version Neurons, Neurons++ extends the paradigm from video-only reconstruction to visual-stream reconstruction for both images and videos, and replaces parallel task conditioning with serial cortex-inspired decoding. The model first uses image-fMRI pretraining to obtain a stable spatial-semantic initialization, and then performs subject-specific fine-tuning for image and video reconstruction separately. The serial design organizes fMRI-derived representations into explicit spatial, semantic, language-level, and temporal cues. Extensive experiments show that Neurons++ achieves state-of-the-art video reconstruction and competitive image reconstruction across standard benchmarks. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, neural-to-visual analyses indicate that Neurons++ exhibits qualitative directional controllability under neural perturbation, that its fMRI encoder read-out is sensitive to stimulus-relevant cortical features, and that it supports ROI-guided semantic modulation. Code and model weights are available at: https://github.com/xmed-lab/NeuronsPlusPlus.

</details>


### [111] [Shortcut Learning in Geospatial Foundation Model Embeddings for Cross-Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13766)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究地理空间基础模型在灾害损失评估中的"捷径学习"问题：当留出整个灾害事件进行评估时，模型性能显著下降，说明其依赖事件/场景特征而非可迁移证据；简单的特征级变换可提升对未见灾害的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型被用于灾害损失评估，但在熟悉灾害上的高精度可能掩盖捷径学习——模型依据事件或场景特征进行预测，而非依据能够迁移到新灾害的证据。因此需要研究在未见灾害上的真实泛化能力。

Method: 研究TerraMind、Prithvi-EO-2.0、DOFA和DINOv3的嵌入表示，在多灾害数据集xView2和BRIGHT上，采用留出整个灾害事件的评估方式（而非随机划分图像块）。设计四组测试考察预测所依赖的信息：事件与获取背景、灾前/灾后图像输入、建筑数量、图像配对正确性。并测试仅用训练灾害学习的特征变换能否提升对未见灾害的泛化。

Result: 留出整个灾害使xView2平均平衡准确率从0.807降至0.638，BRIGHT从0.818降至0.568。灾前图像仍具预测性；仅给出标注建筑数量的分类器在xView2和BRIGHT上分别达0.629和0.653。灾前+灾后特征组合在xView2上平均表现最佳，但在BRIGHT上未优于仅灾前特征。在同一灾害与损害类别内打乱图像配对仅造成轻微损失。特征变换可提升性能，xView2上监督对比学习最佳，BRIGHT上PCA最佳。

Conclusion: 在未见域上进行评估对衡量模型鲁棒性至关重要；简单的特征级变换能够提升对未见灾害的泛化能力，同时揭示了现有地理空间基础模型存在明显的捷径学习问题。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can support disaster damage assessment, but high accuracy on familiar disasters may hide shortcut learning: prediction from event or scene characteristics rather than evidence that transfers to a new disaster. We study embeddings from TerraMind, Prithvi-EO-2.0, DOFA, and DINOv3 on the multi-disaster datasets xView2 and BRIGHT. Holding out an entire disaster, instead of randomly splitting image tiles, reduces mean balanced accuracy from 0.807 to 0.638 on xView2 and from 0.818 to 0.568 on BRIGHT. Four sets of tests examine what information supports prediction: event and acquisition context, before/after image inputs, building count, and correct image pairing. Pre-event images remain predictive, and a classifier given only the annotated number of buildings reaches balanced accuracy of 0.629 and 0.653. Combining before/after features performs best on average on xView2, but does not improve over pre-only features on BRIGHT. Shuffling image pairs within the same disaster and damage class causes modest losses. Motivated by these findings, we test whether feature transformations learned only from training disasters improve generalization to unseen disasters. These transformations improve performance, with supervised contrastive learning performing best on xView2 and PCA on BRIGHT. Our results highlight the importance of evaluation on unseen domains for assessing model robustness and show that simple feature-level transformations can improve generalization. Code and experiments can be found at https://github.com/alishibli97/gfm-shortcut-learning.

</details>


### [112] [READ: Reading the Selective Scan for Faithful Video Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13776)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Piotr Kubaty,Arkadiusz Lewicki,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免参数归因方法READ，在分类器读取位置展开循环，单次前向即得时空相关性图，效果媲美高成本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性状态空间模型适合长序列视频建模，但其递归计算使得难以判断哪些视频token驱动了预测；现有归因方法需额外反向传播或反复模型评估，成本远高于其试图解释的前向推理。

Method: READ在分类器读取的位置展开递归，将循环状态中累积的贡献回溯到对最终预测有贡献的视频token；以分类器读出为归因锚点，产生单一时空相关性图，可直接获得帧级与区域级相关性，且仅需一次前向传播。

Result: 尽管只需一次前向传播，READ在忠实度与定位上可与成本高得多的归因方法竞争；避免现有方法的系统性相关性偏差，并在不同数据集与骨干网络上恢复出有意义的时序与类别特定结构。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间模型底层的递归不仅是高效推理机制，也是其预测可解释证据的直接来源。

Abstract: Selective state-space models are an efficient approach to long-sequence video modeling. However, their recurrent computation makes it difficult to determine which video tokens drive a prediction. Existing attribution methods often require additional backward passes or repeated model evaluations, making them substantially more expensive than the forward inference they aim to explain. In this work, we introduce READ, a parameter-free attribution method for selective state-space models that unrolls the recurrence at the position read by the classifier. READ traces the contribution accumulated in the recurrent state back to the video tokens that contribute to the final prediction. By anchoring attribution at the classifier's readout, it produces a single spatiotemporal relevance map from which frame- and region-level relevance can be obtained directly. Despite requiring only one forward pass, READ achieves competitive faithfulness and localisation compared with substantially more expensive attribution methods. Experimental results show that READ avoids the systematic relevance biases of existing methods while recovering meaningful temporal and class-specific structure across datasets and backbones. More broadly, our results show that the recurrence underlying selective state-space models is not only an efficient mechanism for inference, but also a direct source of interpretable evidence for their predictions.

</details>


### [113] [Detection of extremely rare dead cells in fluorescence microscopy images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13780)
*Prateek Verma,Benjamin Butter,Jian Zhang,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种计算方法（启发式、分类、异常检测）在荧光显微图像中检测凋亡/死亡细胞的效果，发现基于图像特征的深度学习分类（ResNet-18）和基于数值特征的分类（XGBoost）表现最佳，但该问题整体仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微图像中死细胞的自动检测对表型筛选和药效评估至关重要，但由于信号变异、光学伪影、多通道共定位模糊、核边界标记与凋亡标记在时空上不重合，以及标记积累动力学因细胞和凋亡阶段而异导致阈值不可靠，自动化识别仍面临挑战。

Method: 作者在258张图像（含65,527个细胞/核，243个手工标注的死细胞）上系统比较三类方法：(1)启发式方法（强度阈值、分布、相关性、领域引导）；(2)分类方法，包括数值特征（Logistic回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）和图像特征（ResNet-18、EfficientNet-B0）；(3)异常检测，包括数值特征（孤立森林、单类SVM、PCA重建）和图像特征（卷积自编码器、Deep SVDD）。评价指标为PR-AUC、精确率、召回率、F1和macro-F1。

Result: 基于图像特征的分类（深度学习）和基于数值特征的分类（传统方法）表现最佳，其中ResNet-18和XGBoost显著优于手工设计的启发式方法和异常检测方法。

Conclusion: 该生物医学问题即使采用最先进的计算与学习技术仍极具挑战性。作者希望为研究者提供有效的决策支持工具，既可用于全自动检测，也可作为人机协同的辅助手段。

Abstract: Dead cell detection in fluorescence microscopy is essential for phenotypic screening and drug efficacy assessment. However, automated identification remains challenging due to signal variability, optical artifacts, and colocalization ambiguities across multiple channels. Markers that define nuclear boundaries do not neatly coincide spatially or temporally with apoptotic markers. Furthermore, marker accumulation kinetics vary across individual cells and apoptosis stages, making universal thresholds unreliable. We present a systematic comparison of several computational approaches for detecting apoptotic cells in nucleus-stained specimens: (1) heuristic methods using intensity thresholds, distributions, correlations, and domain guidance; (2) classification using numerical features (Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost and LightGBM) and image features (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0); and (3) anomaly detection, also using numerical features (Isolation Forest, one-class SVM and PCA reconstruction) and image features (convolutional autoencoders and Deep SVDD). Using a dataset of 258 images containing 65,527 cells/nuclei across multiple scenes with 243 manually annotated ground truth dead cells, we evaluate each method on metrics such as PR-AUC, Precision, Recall, F1-score, and macro-F1. Our results indicate that classification using image features (deep learning) and numerical features (traditional) achieved the best performance, with ResNet-18 and XGBoost significantly outperforming manually designed heuristics and anomaly detection methods. Our findings demonstrate that such biomedical problems remain highly challenging to solve even with the state-of-the-art computational and learning techniques. We aim to provide an effective decision-support tool to researchers while tackling such problems either fully-automatically or in a human-in-the-loop setting as an aide to domain scientists.

</details>


### [114] [SimVTA: Simulation-Grounded Video-Trajectory Alignment for Driving World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13781)
*Changhui Jing,Haochen Tian,Hongchen Li,Tianyu Li,Caojun Wang,Zihao Zhang,Yanhao Liu,Chengen Xie,Bailin Li,Hao Zhao,Zhenjie Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimVTA 是一个基于仿真的后训练框架，通过将轨迹优化与视频监督结合，为驾驶世界动作模型提供轨迹匹配的视觉监督，显著提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶日志仅为记录的轨迹提供未来视频，而强化学习探索的替代轨迹缺乏对应的视觉监督，导致模型难以获得与轨迹匹配的视觉指导。

Method: 对每个观测采样候选轨迹，利用真实世界模拟器（World Engine）结合 3D 高斯泼溅重建场景，为高奖励候选渲染未来视频；在有限渲染预算下优先监督高误差未来；用 GRPO 优化轨迹规划，并在匹配视频上用流匹配损失更新共享表征。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 上，PDMS 从 86.2 提升至 91.3（+5.1）；NAVSIM v2 达 88.2 EPDMS；HUGSIM 闭环平均 HD-Score 为 38.8。消融实验表明，用视频监督更新共享表征可改善规划，优先监督高误差未来可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: SimVTA 通过仿真渲染轨迹匹配的未来视频，为替代轨迹提供独立于模型自身预测的监督，验证了视频监督与轨迹优化结合在驾驶世界动作模型后训练中的有效性；推理时无需模拟器调用。

Abstract: Driving world action models (WAMs) learn from paired trajectories and future observations. Post-training with reinforcement learning allows the policy to explore alternative trajectories and improve planning using driving rewards. However, driving logs contain future videos only for the recorded trajectories, leaving these alternatives without corresponding visual supervision. Thus, we introduce SimVTA, a simulation-grounded post-training framework that combines trajectory optimization with video supervision. First, we sample candidate trajectories for each observation and use a real-world simulator (e.g., World Engine) to render future videos for high-reward candidates in real-world scenes reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). This provides trajectory-matched supervision independent of the model's own predictions. Under a limited rendering budget, we prioritize video supervision for higher-error futures among these rendered candidates. Finally, we optimize trajectory planning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and use a flow-matching loss on the matched videos to update shared representations. At inference time, the model predicts trajectories directly, without simulator calls. On NAVSIM v1, applying SimVTA to a WAM initialization improves PDMS from 86.2 to 91.3 (+5.1). It achieves 88.2 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and an average HD-Score of 38.8 in closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM. Ablations show that updating shared representations with video supervision improves planning, while prioritizing higher-error futures among high-reward trajectories further improves performance.

</details>


### [115] [SpatialASM: Internalizing Active Mentalization for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13832)
*Yuhong Liu,Shenxi Wu,Junyi Chi,Beichen Zhang,Xuanlang Dai,Jiaqi Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialASM 框架，通过训练时的空间模拟将主动空间心智化（ASM）能力内化到大型视觉语言模型中，在七个空间推理基准上提升了多种 LVLM 主干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在解决复杂空间推理任务时会主动从任务相关视角重新审视场景并重建空间布局，但当前 LVLM 缺乏这种主动思考能力（即主动空间心智化 ASM），且空间思维链（CoT）监督数据稀缺。

Method: 提出 SpatialASM 可扩展框架：使用异我中心（allocentric）的新视角生成器将假设视角锚定在视觉证据上，并产生两类互补监督——i）显式 ASM 轨迹，教模型何时及如何自适应调用视角规划与想象；ii）自动构建的换位思考任务，强化以视角为条件的场景想象。

Result: 在七个空间基准（涵盖感知导向的空间理解到多图、跨视角、视角变换推理）上，SpatialASM 在四种 LVLM 主干上均取得空间推理性能提升，同时保留原有通用视觉能力，并提高了七个基准的平均准确率。

Conclusion: SpatialASM 有效内化了主动空间心智化能力，且 ASM 轨迹的合成提供了一条可扩展路径，缓解空间思维链监督稀缺的问题，激发主动视角探索与心理场景模拟。

Abstract: When solving complex spatial reasoning tasks, ordinary humans naturally revisit the scenes from task-relevant viewpoints and mentally reconstruct the spatial layouts from those viewpoints. But can Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) proactively think in the same way? We term this ability Active Spatial Mentalization (ASM), an intrinsic component of visual intelligence that remains substantially underdeveloped in current LVLMs. To bridge this gap, we introduce SpatialASM, a scalable framework to internalize ASM into LVLMs through training-time spatial simulation. SpatialASM employs an allocentric novel-view generator to ground hypothetical viewpoints in visual evidence and produces two complementary forms of supervision: i) explicit ASM trajectories that teach LVLMs when and how to adaptively invoke view planning and imagination during spatial reasoning, and ii) automatically-constructed perspective-taking tasks that further strengthens viewpoint-conditioned scene imagination. On seven benchmarks spanning relatively perception-oriented spatial understanding to more challenging tasks requiring multi-image, cross-view and viewpoint-transformation reasoning, SpatialASM yields performance gain on LVLMs of four LVLM backbones in spatial reasoning while retaining their original general vision capabilities. The experimental results demonstrate that our approach improves average accuracy across the seven spatial benchmarks. And the synthesis of ASM trajectories provides a scalable pathway to alleviate the scarcity of spatial Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision for eliciting active viewpoint exploration and mental scene simulation.

</details>


### [116] [Event-based Action Recognition by Pre-Training on Multi-Frame Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13856)
*Emma Norrby,Tomas Wilkinson,Per Nyblom,David Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种多帧事件表示结合掩码自编码预训练的视觉Transformer方法，显著提升长时复杂事件序列的动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机能捕捉快速运动并提升隐私保护，使事件动作识别受到关注。但基于帧的事件表示在短序列上表现良好，面对更长更复杂的事件序列时判别能力急剧下降，需要新的表示与预训练方法来应对。

Method: 提出多帧事件表示以替代单帧表示；采用掩码自编码框架预训练视觉Transformer，使用稀疏感知掩码算法和token选择机制以提升下游性能并控制计算复杂度；在解码器中引入多个掩码token及位置感知注意力机制。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长时复杂事件序列上表现优异，在THU-EACT-50 CHL和DailyDVS-200数据集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 多帧表示配合掩码自编码预训练、稀疏感知掩码、token选择与位置感知注意力机制，能有效提升长时复杂事件序列的动作识别性能。

Abstract: Action recognition is a crucial task in domains like robotics, patient monitoring, or surveillance. Recently, event-based action recognition has attracted much attention due to the event camera's ability to capture fast motions and its improved privacy over conventional vision cameras. While frame-based event representations work well for short event sequences, their discriminative ability declines sharply with longer and more complex event sequences. In this work, we introduce a multi-frame representation that greatly improves recognition performance over its single-frame counterpart. We adopt a masked auto-encoding framework to pre-train a Vision Transformer using a sparsity-aware masking algorithm and token selection mechanism to improve downstream performance and control computational complexity. We also introduce multiple mask tokens for the decoder with a position-aware attention mechanism, leading to further improved performance. Our proposed method shows impressive performance for challenging long-duration and complex event sequences, yielding state-of-the-art performance on THU-EACT-50 CHL and DailyDVS-200.

</details>


### [117] [Revisiting the Task of Building Damage Assessment by Satellite Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13857)
*Anthony Cioppa,Virgile Blua,Sébastien Piérard,Pietro Ceccato,Marc Van Droogenbroeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理重新审视建筑物损毁评估（BDA）任务，围绕数据、评估与方法三大支柱展开，提出传感器无关、基于实例且容忍缺失灾前影像的任务定义，指出现有单一聚合评分评估的脆弱性并提出基于排名的多分数评估协议，同时回顾从专用深度网络到地球观测基础模型的方法，并讨论如何组织BDA挑战赛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震、热带风暴、洪水等自然灾害对建筑物造成严重破坏，快速可靠的损毁地图对应急响应至关重要。然而，尽管BDA作为遥感、计算机视觉与机器学习的交叉领域已有十年研究热潮，并发布了多个大规模数据集，该领域仍缺乏共同基础，任务定义、评估方式和数据组织均不统一。

Method: 本文是一篇综述/立场性章节，从第一性原理出发，按数据、评估、方法三条主线组织：（1）梳理候选任务形式，论证采用传感器无关、基于实例且容忍缺失灾前影像的定义；（2）按传感模态、时序配置和地理覆盖范围回顾现有数据集；（3）分析现有评估实践的理论脆弱性，提出基于排名的多分数评估协议；（4）回顾从任务专用深度网络到地球观测基础模型的方法；（5）讨论BDA挑战赛的组织方式，包括交叉验证、竞赛和私有数据共享机制。

Result: 分析表明，当前主流的单一聚合评分评估在理论上较为脆弱，需要更信息丰富的排名式多分数协议；现有方法从未见事件和区域的泛化仍是未解决的难题；数据集在传感模态、时序配置和地理覆盖上各有侧重且缺乏统一标准。

Conclusion: BDA领域需要建立共享基础：统一的任务定义、更完善的评估协议、对泛化能力的持续关注，以及通过合理的挑战赛组织（交叉验证、竞赛、私有数据共享）在开发者与用户之间建立信任。

Abstract: Natural disasters such as earthquakes, tropical storms, and floods cause extensive damage to buildings, and rapid, reliable damage maps are essential to guide emergency response. Automating this task, referred to as building damage assessment (BDA), from satellite imagery, has become an active research topic at the intersection of remote sensing, computer vision, and machine learning, particularly since supervised deep learning methods were shown to outperform earlier handcrafted approaches. Yet, despite a decade of intense activity and the release of several large-scale datasets, the field lacks a shared foundation. This chapter revisits the task of BDA from first principles, organized around three pillars: data, evaluation, and methods. We first clarify the candidate task for- mulations and argue for a sensor-agnostic, instance-based definition that tolerates missing pre-event imagery. We then review existing datasets along the axes of sensing modality, temporal configuration, and geographic coverage. On the evaluation side, we show that prevailing practices, largely based on a single aggregate score, are theoretically fragile, and we lay the foundations of a more informative, ranking-based, multi-score protocol. We further review methods ranging from task-specific deep networks to recent Earth observation foundation models, noticing that generalization to unseen events and regions remains an open difficulty. Finally, we discuss how these elements can inform the organization of a BDA challenge, including cross-validation, challenge, and private data-sharing setups designed to build trust between developers and users.

</details>


### [118] [LapMerge: Differentiable Exact-Budget Token Merging in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13858)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Aleksandra Nowak,Tomasz Steifer,Łukasz Struski,Szymon Płotka,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LapMerge 提出一种可微分的序列令牌合并算子，用学习的质量分布把序列划分为恰好 N 个等质量段，并以 O(M+N) 的正反向开销实现精确输出预算、端到端可微与线性复杂度兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉与语言模型的令牌序列越来越长，压缩令牌是降低下游成本的主要手段。但现有令牌压缩面临根本性权衡：选取（丢弃）类方法会丢失信息，而合并类方法要么依赖逐对令牌匹配，要么需要 M×N 的令牌-输出交互，且难以同时兼顾精确预算、可微性与效率。

Method: LapMerge 是一个面向有序序列的可微分合并算子：通过学习得到的质量分布，在拉普拉斯核密度下将序列划分为恰好 N 个等质量片段；再用前缀/后缀扫描对每个片段做聚合，使前向与反向传播均为 O(M+N)。预算可改变而不影响算子参数，学习到的质量还可作为令牌级预算分配图。

Result: 在视觉与视觉-语言任务上，LapMerge 与强令牌压缩器持平或更优，在长序列下最多快两个数量级；当令牌顺序体现空间或时间局部性时效果尤为突出，既可作为即插即用的压缩模块，也可作为可训练架构组件。

Conclusion: LapMerge 通过等质量分段与线性扫描的合并机制，同时实现了精确输出预算、端到端可微和线性开销，突破了以往压缩器必须相互取舍的三者困境，为长序列视觉-语言模型提供高效且通用的令牌缩减方案。

Abstract: Modern vision and language models process ever longer token sequences, and reducing them is the main lever on downstream cost. Token compression, however, faces a fundamental trade-off: selecting tokens discards information, while existing merging methods preserve it only through pairwise token-to-token matching or $MN$ token-to-output interactions. We introduce LapMerge, a differentiable merging operator that resolves this trade-off for ordered sequences: a learned mass distribution partitions the sequence into exactly $N$ equal-mass segments under a Laplace-kernel density, and prefix/suffix scans aggregate each segment, so both the forward and the backward pass run in $\mathcal{O}(M+N)$ time. This combination - an exact output budget, end-to-end differentiability, and linear cost - is precisely what previous reducers trade off against one another. The budget can be changed without changing operator parameters, and the learned mass doubles as a token-level map of where the budget is allocated. Across vision and vision-language tasks, LapMerge matches or improves on strong token reducers while being up to two orders of magnitude faster at large sequence lengths; it is particularly effective when token order reflects spatial or temporal locality, and works both as a drop-in reduction module and as a trainable architectural component.

</details>


### [119] [Make It Flow: Animating Water in 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13860)
*Kjell Keune,Florian Hahlbohm,Jannis Möller,Susana Castillo,Martin Eisemann,Marcus Magnor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需动态采集或物理模拟、即可在静态3DGS场景中为水体添加动画的方法：通过估计2D光流并将其提升为一致的3D欧拉运动场，结合电影图（cinemagraph）式算法对高斯基元进行形变，生成时间上无缝循环的动态效果，同时保持实时渲染与自由视角浏览能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显式3D场景表示（如3DGS）虽能实现高质量实时新视角合成，但均假设场景为静态，限制了真实世界动态现象的真实感。对于人类、车辆等相干运动可被捕获重建，但流动水体等高频效应在随意采集条件下难以处理，因为无法实现密集多视角、高速采集。因此作者希望在不需要动态采集或物理仿真的前提下，让静态3DGS场景中的水“动起来”。

Method: 方法从参考图像或模型渲染结果中估计2D光流，并将其提升为一个一致的3D欧拉运动场；随后采用受cinemagraph启发的算法，将该运动场作用于高斯基元的形变，从而生成时间上无缝循环的动画，同时保留3DGS的实时渲染性能与自由视角导航能力。

Result: 作者在全新采集的野外瀑布场景数据集上进行评估，结果表明该方法在视觉质量和计算效率两方面均明显优于此前的动态高斯泼溅（dynamic Gaussian splatting）和3D cinemagraph生成工作。

Conclusion: 该方法可在静态3DGS场景中为水体添加视觉上合理的运动，无需动态捕获或物理模拟，既保留了实时渲染性能，又显著提升感知真实感，并支持场景内的自由视角浏览，为在随意采集条件下实现动态化3D场景提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Explicit 3D scene representations such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have recently enabled high-quality, real-time novel view synthesis. However, these methods assume static scenes, limiting realism in the presence of dynamic phenomena ubiquitous in real-world environments. While coherent motion (e.g., humans or vehicles) can be captured and reconstructed, high-frequency effects such as flowing water remain intractable under casual capture conditions, where dense multi-view, high-speed acquisition is not feasible. We present a method to animate water in static 3DGS scenes, enabling visually plausible motion without requiring dynamic capture or physical simulation. Our approach estimates 2D optical flow from reference images or model renderings and lifts it into a consistent 3D Eulerian motion field. We propose a cinemagraph-inspired algorithm that applies this motion field to deform Gaussian primitives, producing temporally seamless looping animations. The resulting representation retains real-time rendering performance, significantly enhances perceptual realism, and preserves free-viewpoint navigation within the scene. We evaluate our method on a newly captured dataset of in-the-wild waterfall scenes and demonstrate clear improvements over prior work in dynamic Gaussian splatting and 3D cinemagraph generation in visual quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Tracking the Invisible: Self-Supervised Wheel Track Detection with Thermal-RGB Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13872)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本、自监督的方法，利用同步 RGB-I（可见光+长波红外）图像检测雪覆盖道路上的安全关键车轮轨迹，并通过 GNSS 数据自动生成训练标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行驶区域检测方法多针对一般可通行性估计，在雪覆盖道路上表现不足；而沿既有车轮轨迹行驶能带来更好的牵引力、可预测的车辆动力学和已验证的路径稳定性，因此需要专门检测车轮轨迹。

Method: 将记录的 GNSS 数据中未来车轮轨迹投影以自动生成训练标签，实现自监督；设计神经网络，利用 4 通道 RGB-I 图像在极具挑战的白茫茫路面条件下识别车轮轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在视觉单调、缺乏特征的不利环境中有效，为冬季自主驾驶提供了稳健且经济的替代方案。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效检测雪地车轮轨迹，并发布了新的 ThermalTrack 数据集。

Abstract: Accurate detection of drivable area is essential for autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. While state-of-the-art methods focus on general traversability estimation, they prove inadequate for snow-covered roads where following existing wheel tracks offers crucial advantages: improved traction, predictable vehicle dynamics, and verified path stability. To address this, we propose a low-cost, self-supervised approach detecting safety-critical wheel tracks using synchronized RGB-I (RGB + Long-Wave Infrared) imagery. Our method automatically generates training labels by projecting future wheel trajectories from recorded GNSS data. A neural network is designed to identify wheel tracks with the 4-channel RGB-I images from challenging, white-out road surfaces. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in visually monotonous and featureless adverse environment, offering a robust and cost-effective alternative for winter autonomy. The newly introduced ThermalTrack dataset is available at: https://digitalcommons.mtu.edu/thermaltrack/.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond the Snowflakes: Disentangling Weather Effects from Confounding Meta-Factors in LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13891)
*Yiming Yang,Jeremy P. Bos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 降雪增强而非削弱激光雷达目标检测精度，因为积雪导致物体点云数量显著增加，其正面几何增强效应超过了雪花噪声的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍将雪视为降低激光雷达感知性能的大气噪声，但缺乏对物体级数据的细致评估。作者希望通过细粒度评估揭示天气严重程度与检测精度之间的真实关系，并指出按宽泛天气等级评估感知系统的潜在误导性。

Method: 在 WADS-3D 和 CADC 数据集上进行细粒度评估，结合消融实验与元信息分析，将大气噪声效应与表面累积效应解耦，并分析物体点云数量、反射特性等物理因素。

Result: 检测精度随降雪强度增加而持续提升；雪花噪声虽有负面影响，但被物体点云数量显著增加带来的正面效应所掩盖。该点云增加源于积雪增大车辆表面积以及镜面反射向漫反射的转变。

Conclusion: 使用宽泛天气严重程度分箱、忽略物体级相关数据的评估方法会得出关于检测器鲁棒性的误导性结论；准确评估需将大气噪声与表面累积效应解耦，未来需进一步验证相关物理模型。

Abstract: Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) is critical for autonomous perception, yet its performance is known to degrade in adverse weather. While existing literature models snow as atmospheric noise that degrades performance, our granular evaluation on the WADS-3D and CADC datasets reveals a counter-intuitive phenomenon: detection accuracy consistently improves as snow intensity increases. Through ablation study and meta-information analysis, we reconcile this by confirming that while falling snow acts as noise, its negative impact is outweighed by a dominant, concurrent factor: significantly increased object point count. We hypothesize that this object point count boost acts as a geometric enhancement, driven by snow accumulation increasing vehicle surface area and a shift from specular to diffuse reflectance. Our findings demonstrate that evaluating perception systems using broad weather-severity bins without accounting for correlated object-level data can lead to misleading conclusions about detector robustness. We conclude that accurate evaluation requires decoupling atmospheric noise from surface accumulation effects and outline future research to validate these physical models. The WADS-3D dataset is available at: https://digitalcommons.mtu.edu/wads-3d/.

</details>


### [122] [Where to Look Matters: Rethinking Sub-Volume Sampling in 3D Medical Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13915)
*Junkai Liu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出3D医学图像自监督预训练中随机子体积采样忽视解剖先验与空间冗余问题，提出将子体积采样重新定义为"where-to-look"问题，并设计了即插即用的解剖感知、信息量、多样性引导的探针库框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学图像SSL通常使用随机子体积采样，但随机裁剪隐含地假定所有空间区域价值相等，忽略了解剖先验和空间冗余导致的区域价值差异。作者通过可控的裁剪质量操控实验证明，更好的子体积能带来更好的下游迁移效果，说明观察策略是3D医学SSL中被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 将子体积采样重新视为"where-to-look"问题，提出plug-and-play的\method框架，用离线粗到细的探针库替代随机裁剪，引导信号包括解剖感知性、信息量和多样性。实例化为两种变体：轻量级的\lite变体使用几何与图像统计线索，诊断性的\feat变体使用冻结视觉特征，以检验轻量体积内在先验是否足够，或更丰富语义线索是否带来额外价值。

Result: 在多个3D医学SSL骨干网络和下游任务上的大量实验表明，\method-\lite相比随机采样持续提升迁移性能。机制分析将这些收益归因于：更符合解剖结构且更具信息量的观察、减少的空间冗余、更高效的学习动态以及更结构化的表征。

Conclusion: 观察策略是3D医学SSL的关键瓶颈，用解剖感知、信息量、多样性引导的探针库替代随机裁剪是一种有效且即插即用的方案；仅依赖轻量体积内在先验即可稳定提升迁移性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning on 3D medical images commonly relies on random sub-volume sampling during pretraining. However, random cropping implicitly treats all spatial regions as equally worth observing, despite anatomical priors and spatial redundancy that make regions inherently unequal in their value for representation learning. To this end, we first show through controlled crop-quality manipulations that better sub-volumes lead to better downstream transfer, revealing the observation policy as an overlooked bottleneck in 3D medical SSL. Motivated by this finding, we revisit sub-volume sampling as a \emph{where-to-look} problem. We propose \textbf{\method}, a plug-and-play framework that replaces random cropping with an offline coarse-to-fine probe bank guided by \textit{anatomy-awareness}, \textit{informativeness}, and \textit{diversity}. We instantiate \method as a lightweight \lite variant using geometric and image-statistical cues, and a diagnostic \feat variant using frozen visual features, to examine whether lightweight volume-intrinsic priors suffice or richer semantic cues reliably provide additional value. Extensive experiments across multiple 3D medical SSL backbones and downstream tasks demonstrate that \method-\lite consistently improves transfer performance over random sampling. Mechanistic analyses further attribute these gains to more anatomically plausible and informative observations, reduced spatial redundancy, more efficient learning dynamics, and more structured representations.

</details>


### [123] [Can machines reason non-verbally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13925)
*Shaheer U. Saeed,Zion Tse,Gregory Slabaugh,Daniel C. Alexander,Yipeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenVerOpt 框架，将生成、验证与优化统一为机器推理流程，通过生成候选解、验证正确性并迭代精炼，实现逐样本优化，在视觉非语言推理任务上超越现有系统甚至人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器虽能解方程、遵循逻辑和进行语言推理，但在需要视觉非语言推理的任务（如完成抽象图形、划分模糊感兴趣区域）上仍表现不佳，即使是 DeepSeek、ChatGPT、Gemini 等先进系统也受限。与可通过逻辑或自动化测试验证的语言推理显式中间步骤不同，视觉推理的中间步骤是隐式的，缺乏明确的验证方法，因此难以建模，成为当前机器智能的根本局限。

Method: 提出 GenVerOpt 通用框架，把生成、验证和优化统一为一条推理流水线：生成候选解、验证其正确性、迭代精炼，从而实现逐样本优化，而非仅依赖模型训练中捕获的数据集层面趋势。该过程模拟人类"设想多种可能、评估并精炼直至得到正确解"的解题方式。

Result: 在多个领域展现超越现有系统的能力：在抽象智力测试题上超过人类，在前列腺癌分割上媲美临床医生，在肝血管重建上超越专家，在肿瘤边界对齐上优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明机器推理可以超越语言，视觉非语言推理可作为更灵活、更具泛化能力的智能基础。

Abstract: Modern machines can solve equations, follow logic and reason through language, yet they still struggle with problems that require visual non-verbal reasoning. This is evident even in advanced systems such as DeepSeek, ChatGPT and Gemini, which remain limited on visual tasks such as completing abstract patterns or delineating ambiguous regions-of-interest. Unlike explicit intermediate steps in verbal reasoning which can be verified through logic or automated tests, visual reasoning involves implicit intermediate steps with no clear methods for verification. Non-verbal reasoning is therefore challenging to model and, as a result, remains a fundamental limitation of current machine intelligence. Here we introduce GenVerOpt, a general framework that unifies generation, verification and optimisation into a reasoning pipeline for machines. By generating candidate solutions, verifying their correctness, and iteratively refining them, GenVerOpt enables per-sample optimisation rather than relying solely on dataset-level trends captured during model training. This process mirrors human problem-solving where multiple possibilities are imagined, evaluated, and refined until a correct solution emerges. Across diverse domains, GenVerOpt demonstrates capabilities beyond existing systems: it surpasses humans on abstract IQ puzzles, matches clinicians in prostate cancer segmentation, exceeds experts in liver vessel reconstruction and outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in tumour boundary alignment. These results show that machine reasoning can extend beyond language, establishing visual non-verbal reasoning as a foundation for more flexible and generalisable intelligence.

</details>


### [124] [Hide and Seek: HIDE Concepts to SEEK explanations for black box models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13935)
*Bhanu Tokas,Rahul Nair,Rockwell Jackson,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hide and Seek（HnS），一种无需模型权重或标注概念数据集的即插即用概念级归因方法，通过检索、定位、遮蔽并观测黑盒模型输出变化实现忠实归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的归因方法仅适用于分类与回归模型，且需要访问模型权重或大规模概念标注数据，多数方法无法提供图像级归因，限制了在黑盒视觉模型上的应用。

Method: HnS给定一个概念和图像集，先用视觉-语言模型检索与概念对齐的图像，再在每张图像中定位该概念区域并将其遮蔽，最后通过比较原始图像与遮蔽图像在黑盒模型输出上的变化来度量归因，从而直接依据模型行为获得忠实的局部归因。

Result: 在CelebA和COCO-Stuff数据集上，HnS与真实归因的一致性优于现有方法；并在图像描述和卫星图像分割等任务上展示了适用性，而这些任务此前没有可用的概念归因方法。

Conclusion: HnS无需模型权重或标注概念数据集，可解释多种视觉模型并支持局部归因，拓展了概念归因方法的应用范围。

Abstract: Interpreting machine learning model predictions is a central challenge, particularly for black-box vision models. Concept-based attribution methods interpret models using human-understandable concepts. Existing concept-based methods can only interpret classification and regression models. They require access to model weights or large concept-annotated datasets, and most methods cannot provide image-level attribution. We propose Hide and Seek (HnS), a new method for concept-level attribution that does not require access to model weights or labeled concept datasets. HnS can interpret a wide range of vision models. Given a concept and image set, HnS retrieves concept-aligned images using a vision-language model, localizes the concept within each image, masks the localized region, and measures attribution from the change in the black-box model's output between the original and masked images. This enables faithful attribution by directly measuring the model's behavior and supporting local attributions. We validated HnS on CelebA and COCO-Stuff. Compared to existing methods, HnS showed better agreement with ground-truth attributions. We demonstrated the applicability of HnS on diverse tasks, including image captioning and satellite image segmentation, where no existing concept attribution method can be used. We provided our anonymous code in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [125] [Images Talk Back: Guiding MMDiTs by Suppressing Image-to-Text Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13941)
*Yucheng Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Shusheng Yang,Jiakang Chen,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态扩散Transformer中图像到文本（I2T）反馈的作用，发现其会保留演化图像中的错误表征。据此提出语义反馈引导（SFG）与iSFG方法，通过抑制I2T反馈、强化文本到图像条件来提升生成对齐度。在多个骨干网络及视频生成任务中均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer中，演化图像通过联合注意力更新内部文本表征，进而引导后续图像更新。这种I2T反馈究竟是纠正还是保留了对提示的偏差，尚不明确，这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 作者通过干预实验发现I2T反馈会保留图像中已表征的对象与属性（含错误）。据此提出语义反馈引导（SFG）：通过远离辅助分支来抑制I2T反馈影响并削弱文本到图像条件；其改进版iSFG直接抑制I2T反馈并强化文本到图像条件，无需额外模型评估。SFG分支仅在前20%采样步运行。

Result: 在三个图像生成骨干上均提升对齐度与偏好模型评分。FLUX上SFG将GenEval从0.6661提升至0.7479；SD3.5M上iSFG在零额外NFE下较CFG提升+1.33 HPSv2.1。该方法还可迁移至文本到视频和图像到视频生成，提升VBench分数。

Conclusion: I2T反馈倾向于保留而非纠正演化图像中的表征（含错误），通过抑制该反馈可有效改善生成质量。SFG/iSFG在多种任务和骨干上验证了有效性与泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs) allow the evolving image to update internal text representations through joint attention. These representations then guide subsequent image updates, raising a question: does this feedback help correct deviations from the prompt, or preserve them? We find that image-to-text (I2T) feedback can preserve the objects and attributes already represented in the evolving image, including errors. Transplanting I2T messages can rescue failures or disrupt successes. At matched intervention strengths, suppressing the influence of I2T feedback improves generation, whereas opposite controls degrade it. These findings motivate Semantic Feedback Guidance (SFG), which suppresses the influence of I2T feedback by guiding away from an auxiliary branch that strengthens this feedback and weakens text-to-image conditioning. The in-place variant, iSFG, directly suppresses I2T feedback and strengthens text-to-image conditioning, requiring no extra model evaluations. Temporal interventions motivate running the SFG branch during the first 20% of image sampling steps, requiring about $1.2\times$ the base NFE without classifier-free guidance (CFG). Experiments across three image-generation backbones show gains in alignment and preference-model scores. On FLUX, adding SFG to CFG raises GenEval from $0.6661$ to $0.7479$; on SD3.5M, iSFG adds $+1.33$ HPSv2.1 over CFG at zero extra NFE. The control also transfers to text-to-video and image-to-video generation, improving VBench scores. Code is available at https://github.com/yyc1215lll-creator/SFG.

</details>


### [126] [InstantFusion: A Shared Latent Interface for Heterogeneous Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13944)
*Xuanlang Dai,Jiazi Bu,Yujie Zhou,Yuhong Liu,Beichen Zhang,Penghui Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 InstantFusion，一个轻量级共享潜空间接口，可连接异构图像生成器的去噪轨迹，无需为每对模型单独映射，即可实现跨模型状态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型已成为图像生成的主流范式，模型架构与表示日益异构，但跨模型的潜表征对齐与去噪动力学一致性仍缺乏探索。作者发现独立训练的生成器具有相似的潜空间组织，且即使模型未对齐，直接传递中间状态仍能保留粗略视觉内容，这启发了构建通用跨模型接口的想法。

Method: 提出 InstantFusion，通过学习共享潜空间实现异构生成器去噪轨迹的衔接。具体引入模型专属的潜自编码器（LAEs），将不同生成器的中间状态投影到共享潜空间，再解码到目标生成器的潜空间，从而灵活实现跨模型状态转换。共享空间受两个互补目标约束：对齐同一图像在匹配噪声水平下导出的中间状态，以及同步其对应的局部去噪速度。训练完成后可直接在冻结的生成器之间无缝切换去噪过程。

Result: 在加速、多偏好组合、跨架构在线策略蒸馏以及跨模型组合等多种设置下展示了 InstantFusion 的通用性，并取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: InstantFusion 表明异构生成器之间存在可被有效利用的跨模型兼容性，共享潜空间接口为跨模型去噪轨迹衔接提供了无需成对映射的可扩展方案，具备广泛的通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Diffusion/flow models have emerged as the de facto paradigms for image generation, giving rise to a diverse family of modern generators with increasingly heterogeneous architectures and representations. Despite their remarkable generation capabilities, the cross-model alignment of latent representations and the consistency of their underlying denoising dynamics remain underexplored. In this work, we uncover an intriguing cross-model compatibility: independently trained generators exhibit similar latent spatial organization, while directly handing off intermediate states between unaligned models can still preserve coarse visual content. Motivated by these observations, we propose InstantFusion, a lightweight shared latent interface that connects the denoising trajectories of heterogeneous image generators. Specifically, to enable flexible cross-model state translation, model-specific Latent AutoEncoders (LAEs) are introduced to project intermediate states from different generators into a shared latent space and decode them into the latent space of a target generator. This shared space is constrained by two complementary objectives: aligning intermediate states derived from the same image at matched noise levels and synchronizing their corresponding local denoising velocities. Once trained, InstantFusion allows denoising to seamlessly transition across frozen generators without requiring a dedicated mapping for every model pair. The versatility of InstantFusion is demonstrated across acceleration, multi-preference composition, cross-architecture on-policy distillation, and cross-model composition, achieving competitive performance across these diverse settings.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Physically Grounded Articulated 3D Assets: A Multi-Agent Pipeline and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13947)
*Xudong Cai,Ruijie Zhu,Haoliang Shang,Ruining Li,Marc Pollefeys,Shangzhe Wu,Andrea Vedaldi,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Artisan，一种带物理仿真反馈的多智能体关节 3D 资产生成系统，并构建涵盖空间保真、物理合理性、下游任务与整体质量的统一基准，在多模型评测中取得最低碰撞率与最高稳定率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关节 3D 资产生成主要追求外观与几何还原，而忽略物体如何运动与发挥作用。作者希望将目标从"看起来像"推进到"动得对、用得起来"，即生成具备无碰撞运动、重力下稳定性以及准确质量与材料属性的物理可用资产。

Method: 受程序化生成与智能体生成研究启发，作者在 Articraft 基础上扩展出 Artisan：采用多智能体流水线，并引入物理仿真反馈，使生成结果在几何、外观、关节合理性之外，还具备无碰撞运动、重力稳定性和质量/材料属性等更全面特性。同时构建统一基准，覆盖空间保真、物理合理性、下游任务性能与整体质量四个方面。

Result: 基准包含来自 ArtVIP 与 LightWheel 的 100 个参考资产、50 条稳定性提示词、50 个带参考质量的 ABO 物体，并配有多项评测协议。对 10 余个代表性模型进行广泛评测后，Artisan 在空间保真上具有竞争力，同时在所有评测方法中取得最低碰撞率与最高稳定率。

Conclusion: 该系统与基准共同为 3D 生成迈向物理合理、功能可用的资产提供了新的认识与方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we study physically grounded articulated 3D asset generation---moving the goal from reproducing how objects look to capturing how they move and function. Inspired by the literature on procedural and agentic generation, we present Artisan, a system that extends the latest method Articraft by introducing a multi-agent pipeline with physical simulation feedback, enabling comprehensive attributes beyond geometry, appearance, and physically plausible articulation, such as collision-free motion, stability under gravity, and accurate mass and material properties. Furthermore, we construct a unified benchmark spanning four key aspects: spatial fidelity, physical plausibility, downstream task performance, and overall quality. The benchmark encompasses 100 reference assets from ArtVIP and LightWheel, 50 stability prompts, and 50 ABO objects with reference masses, with protocols evaluating various metrics. Through extensive evaluation of more than 10 representative models, Artisan achieves competitive spatial fidelity while attaining the lowest collision rates and highest stability rates among the evaluated methods. Together, our system and benchmark establish insights for advancing 3D generation towards physically plausible, functionally usable assets.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [AutoIndexer: Flexible-Order Decoding by Training Causal Models on Chains of Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12545)
*Shu Ishida,Aliasghar Khani,Tianyu Zhang,The Cong Luong,James Seale Smith,Adam Gaier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoIndexer，一种模拟"编辑链"的生成模型，通过训练和新型解码机制让模型能够事后灵活地插入、替换和删除先前输出中的 token，解决了自回归解码难以处理非因果顺序数据的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码不适合处理非因果顺序的数据（如交叉引用或未来依赖），因为其输出无法事后修正，这与人类迭代式推理不同。扩散 LLM 虽部分缓解此问题，但仍无法从预生成上下文中灵活地插入或删除任意数量的 token。

Method: 设计 AutoIndexer，模拟"编辑链"（Chain of Edits）。模型学习预测用于开启和关闭编辑的标记 token，同时像文本编辑器一样将光标移动到现有 token 序列中的位置，支持插入、替换和删除操作。训练序列通过对真实标签施加逆向链式编辑操作进行扰动生成，模型通过学习撤销这些逆操作来掌握编辑能力。扰动训练使模型对错误上下文具有鲁棒性，并能适应代码纠错等编辑任务。

Result: AutoIndexer 展现出大多数自回归和扩散模型所不具备的生成后上下文编辑能力，同时在标准 LLM 基准测试上性能没有下降。

Conclusion: AutoIndexer 通过编辑链式的训练与解码机制，实现了事后灵活修改先前输出的能力，兼顾了编辑灵活性与标准任务性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding is less suited to non-causally ordered data, e.g., cross-references or future dependencies, since their outputs cannot be revised post hoc, unlike iterative human reasoning. Diffusion LLMs partially mitigate this limitation but still cannot flexibly insert or remove arbitrary numbers of tokens from a pre-generated context. We design AutoIndexer as a generative model with a novel training and decoding mechanism that simulates a Chain of Edits, allowing the model to flexibly modify its prior outputs in hindsight via token insertions, substitutions, and deletions. The model learns to predict marker tokens for opening and closing edits, simultaneously moving a cursor to positions in the existing token sequence, analogous to a text editor. Training sequences are obtained by perturbing ground-truth labels with inverse chained edit operations, and the model learns to edit by undoing those inverses. The perturbation training allows AutoIndexer to be robust to erroneous context and adaptable to editing tasks such as code correction. We demonstrate that our method exhibits post-generation context-editing capabilities that most autoregressive and diffusion models lack without compromising performance on standard LLM benchmarks. The code is available at https://github.com/AutodeskAILab/autoindexer.

</details>


### [129] [SWE-Duel: Towards a Self-Scaling Arena for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12569)
*Wei Minn,Zhensu Sun,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SWE-Duel，一种自我扩展的编码 agent 评测竞技场：任务与判定在评测时动态生成，由两个 agent 互为出题者与解题者，难度随 agent 能力自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态编码基准固定在发布时，任务会随时间泄漏进训练数据，且能力更强的 agent 最终会将其饱和。需要一个内容事先不存在、难度由参与者动态决定的自我扩展式基准。

Method: 构建 SWE-Duel 双 agent 对战竞技场，采用镜像的 Red-Blue 轮替。Red agent 在真实仓库中经三个上下文隔离会话完成：提出并实现新功能及功能测试，随后在该功能中植入隐蔽 bug 并附带封存的 bug 触发测试。PR 需同时满足两个门控条件才被接纳：功能测试与仓库既有测试通过且恰好在 bug 测试上失败；且同一 agent 的新会话能在盲测下修复该 bug（排除不可解任务）。Blue agent 在不知存在 bug 的情况下评审该 PR，仅当其修订版通过全部三类测试才获胜，随后双方交换角色。

Result: 在 5 种语言、15 个仓库、11 个模型-框架组合上进行了 1500 轮锦标赛。GPT-5.5 + Codex CLI 以最强解题者排第一，修复了其评审缺陷的 98.3%；Claude Opus 4.8 + mini-swe-agent 以最强出题者排第二，Red 胜率 27.3%。同一模型族内，新版本撰写的任务击败固定参考解题者的比例远高于旧版本（42.9% 对 4.0%）。

Conclusion: SWE-Duel 无需预先内容即可避免泄漏与饱和，且任务难度随出题能力提升而扩展，初步证据表明该竞技场可作为自我扩展型编码基准。

Abstract: Static coding benchmarks are fixed at release. Over time their tasks leak into training data, and stronger agents eventually saturate them. We argue that a benchmark should instead aim to be self-scaling: its tasks and oracles are created at evaluation time, so no content exists in advance to leak, and its difficulty is set by the participating agents rather than fixed at release. We introduce SWE-Duel, an arena in which two coding agents evaluate each other over mirrored Red-Blue turns. In each turn, the Red agent works on a real repository across three context-isolated sessions. It proposes and implements a new feature with feature tests, then embeds a subtle bug in that feature along with sealed bug-triggering tests. The resulting pull request is admitted only if (1) it passes the feature tests and the repository's existing tests while failing exactly the bug tests, and (2) a fresh session of the same agent can repair the bug blind, which rules out unsolvable tasks. The Blue agent then reviews the pull request without being told a bug exists. It wins only if its revision passes all three test suites, and then the two agents swap roles. We run a tournament among 11 model-harness participants on 15 repositories in five languages for 1,500 turns. The leaders win in different ways. GPT-5.5 with Codex CLI ranks first as the strongest solver, repairing 98.3% of the defects it reviews. Claude Opus 4.8 with mini-swe-agent ranks second as the strongest task author, with a 27.3% Red win rate. Finally, within one model family, tasks written by the newest version defeat a fixed-reference solver far more often than those of older versions (42.9% vs. 4.0%), giving preliminary evidence that task difficulty scales with task authoring capability.

</details>


### [130] [Contextual Correction of Parallel Draftsfor Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12570)
*Roy Eisenstadt,Harel Lidar,Tal Gonen,Omer Luxembourg,Itamar Zimerman,Lior Wolf,Eli David,Ilan Naiman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DCOCO是一种轻量级修正机制，通过并行保留多个候选token并评估相邻草稿位置的上下文兼容性，恢复扩散式草稿中缺失的块内依赖，从而在多种解码与负载条件下实现最高吞吐量，最高达6.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的drafter可并行预测多个未来token，避免自回归生成的顺序开销，但由于草稿块内token在预测时无法获知前序token的真实身份，块内依赖被移除，导致上下文一致性受损。现有顺序修正器在高并发负载下表现不佳。

Method: 提出DCOCO（DFlash Contextual Corrector）。它不直接采用每个位置的最高预测，而是保留多个候选token，并在相邻草稿位置上并行评估其上下文兼容性，从而以低额外成本实现上下文感知的token选择，同时保持并行草稿。

Result: 在4个验证器骨干和10个基准上，DCOCO在每种配置中均取得最高平均吞吐量，涵盖贪婪与随机解码、是否带扩展推理，最高较原始解码加速6.6倍。在并发服务下，4–32并发请求中比最强竞争方法高6–23%，而其他顺序修正器在这些负载下甚至低于未修正的DFlash。

Conclusion: 通过并行上下文评分恢复块内依赖，可在不同解码机制与服务负载下提供一致的加速效果，证明该方法是可行且稳健的。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens that are subsequently verified in parallel by the target model. Recent diffusion-based drafters make this paradigm particularly attractive: deep attention-based neural backbones provide strong predictions, while drafting multiple future positions in parallel avoids the sequential cost of autoregressive generation. This parallelism, however, comes at a cost. Tokens within a draft block are predicted without access to the realized identities of preceding draft tokens, removing intra-block dependencies that would naturally be available under autoregressive generation. We introduce DCOCO (DFlash Contextual Corrector), a lightweight mechanism that restores these dependencies while preserving parallel drafting. Rather than committing to each position's top prediction, DCOCO retains multiple candidate tokens and evaluates their contextual compatibility across adjacent draft positions in parallel, enabling contextually informed token selection at low additional cost. Across four verifier backbones and ten benchmarks, DCOCO achieves the highest average throughput in every evaluated configuration, spanning greedy and stochastic decoding with and without extended reasoning, with speedups of up to 6.6x over vanilla decoding. Its advantage persists under concurrent serving: DCOCO outperforms the strongest competing method by 6-23% across 4-32 concurrent requests, while other sequential correctors fall below uncorrected DFlash at these loads. These results demonstrate that recovering intra-block dependencies through parallel contextual scoring delivers consistent acceleration across decoding regimes and serving loads.

</details>


### [131] [CLUE: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Unsupervised Event Type Induction and Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12580)
*Pratik Maitra,Lauren Kwon,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLUE框架，自动完成事件类型归纳、命名与标注，减少人工本体构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件本体是数据集中事件定义的基础框架，但传统事件类型归纳依赖领域专家制定准则，成本高且易受人为偏见影响，因此需要自动化方法。

Method: 设计新的联合嵌入表示，同时表达事件的动词中心视角和语义视角；使用对比损失引导的聚类进行事件类型归纳；并进行事件命名与细化。

Result: 实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CLUE框架能够用于事件类型归纳、命名和标注，并可缓解人工事件本体创建的需求。

Abstract: Event ontology is the foundational framework for defining events in a dataset. Event type induction generally involves domain experts who define guidelines for creating event types and their names based on the dataset's domain and scope. However, this process is resource-intensive in terms of annotation costs and is prone to human biases. To alleviate the need for manual event ontology creation, we propose the CLUE framework for event type induction, naming, and annotation. Our main contributions in this work are: 1) designing a novel joint embedding representation for expressing both the verb-centric view and the semantic view of events; 2) event type induction using contrastive loss guided clustering; 3) event naming and refinement. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Our code is available at https://github.com/PratikMaitra/CLUE.

</details>


### [132] [One Model to Loop Them All: Elastic Latent Recurrence for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12582)
*Faisal Ladhak,Shantanu Acharya,Santiago Akle Serano,Simeng Sun,Boris Ginsburg,Krishna C Puvvada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ELR训练配方，使循环语言模型在单次循环时即达到非循环同尺寸模型的精度（消除"单循环税"），且额外循环持续提升精度，实现单一检查点在推理时以算力换精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在循环语言模型通过重复共享层块，可用同一检查点改变循环次数来权衡推理算力与精度。但单循环时这类模型通常远逊于同尺寸非循环模型，形成"单循环税"，导致对延迟敏感场景下非循环模型更优。作者希望训练出无此损失的循环模型。

Method: 三项关键设计：(1)带学习步长的门控状态更新；(2)每次优化器更新内混合不同循环次数；(3)各次前向以循环索引与预算为条件。前两项保持单循环精度，第三项降低每次额外循环的损失。混合深度可延后至预训练后期引入以限制训练开销，并采用零初始化条件在切换点加入，使该后期调度比从头混合深度训练损失更低。MoE模型中后续循环复用首循环专家路由，混合深度更新耗时减少11.7%且精度无变化。推理时仅保留最后一循环的注意力KV缓存，将KV显存维持在单循环水平同时保留大部分额外循环收益。

Result: 在4B稠密与30B-A3B MoE混合模型上，ELR单循环在十任务平均上分别低于/高于匹配基线0.52分与1.04分；四循环分别增加7.25与4.75分。贯穿后训练得到Koi-4B，单循环在Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index上估计18分（与Qwen3.5-4B相当），四循环29分。

Conclusion: ELR消除了循环模型的单循环税，使单一检查点可在推理时按需以循环次数换取精度，并在稠密与MoE模型上验证了有效性，产出可部署的Koi-4B模型。

Abstract: A latent recurrent language model applies a shared block of layers repeatedly, so one checkpoint can trade inference compute for accuracy by changing the loop count. At one loop, however, such models typically fall well short of a non-recurrent model of the same size, a one-loop tax that makes a separate non-recurrent model the better choice whenever latency matters. We present Elastic Latent Recurrence (ELR), a recipe for training looped language models without this tax. At one loop, an ELR model costs the same to serve as a non-recurrent model of the same size and, trained on the same data, reaches similar accuracy. Each additional loop then buys more accuracy for more inference compute. Three choices make this work: a gated state update with a learned step size, loop counts mixed within each optimizer update, and conditioning each pass on its loop index and budget. The first two preserve one-loop accuracy, and the third lowers loss at every additional loop. Mixed depths can be introduced late in pretraining to limit the added training cost, and with zero-initialized conditioning added at the switch, this late schedule reaches lower loss than mixed-depth training from scratch. In MoE models, reusing first-loop expert routes on later loops cuts mixed-depth update time by 11.7% with no measurable change in accuracy. At inference, keeping only the last loop's attention KV cache holds attention KV memory at its one-loop level while retaining most of the gain from additional loops. On 4B dense and 30B-A3B MoE hybrid models, ELR at one loop is 0.52 points below and 1.04 points above its matched baseline on a ten-task average, and four loops add 7.25 and 4.75 points. Carried through post-training, the recipe yields Koi-4B, a single checkpoint that scores an estimated 18 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at one loop, matching Qwen3.5-4B, and 29 at four loops.

</details>


### [133] [Reflective Prompt Optimization of a Deep-Research Agent: A Topic-Disjoint, Multi-Seed Protocol for Measuring Transferable Gains and Prompt-Level Overfitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12601)
*Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将反思式提示进化（GEPA）用于多智能体深度研究智能体的系统提示，在主题不相交划分下，引用奖励从0.702升至0.781（提升0.079，95%CI [0.051,0.107]），三个随机种子可复现。分析表明记忆化答案并非增益来源，分解式逐维反馈才是迁移的关键因果因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化相比强化学习更便宜、产物可读，但在事实性任务上存在标量奖励强化学习没有的失效模式：优化器读取训练样本后，可能把答案写进提示，使分布内收益实为记忆而非可迁移行为。因此需要在严格隔离的评估设置下检验提示优化的真实迁移性。

Method: 对多智能体深度研究智能体的两个系统提示应用反思式提示进化（GEPA），在训练、验证、测试主题完全不共享的 topic-disjoint 划分下评估。通过 bootstrap 置信区间与三种子重复验证增益；用控制消融检验记忆化内容的作用；改变验证器（verifier）设置，对比分解式逐维反馈与坍缩为单一标量奖励的效果；并用家族外评判器与手写版配方做交叉验证。

Result: 留出集引用奖励提升0.079（0.702→0.781），bootstrap 95%CI [0.051,0.107]，三种子均值提升0.107。获胜提示近一半为记忆化答案键，但消融显示即便在训练问题上该内容也不带来奖励。验证集与留出集的差距源于小样本选择而非记忆化。分解式逐维反馈是增益迁移的因果要素，坍缩为单一标量奖励会使留出迁移为负。增益在家族外评判器下依然存在，且手写版配方大体可复现。

Conclusion: 对提示优化器的可信评估需要留出主题、多种子实验与验证器消融；逐维分解反馈是提示优化产生可迁移增益的关键。作者公开候选提示、谱系差异、图表与分析。

Abstract: Prompt optimization is an attractive alternative to reinforcement learning for adapting language-model agents, since it is cheap and produces human-readable artifacts. On factual tasks, however, it carries a failure mode that scalar-reward reinforcement learning does not. An optimizer that reads the training examples can write their answers into the prompt, so an in-distribution gain may be memorization rather than transferable behaviour. We apply reflective prompt evolution (GEPA) to the two system prompts of a multi-agent deep-research agent and evaluate it under a topic-disjoint split in which no topic is shared across train, validation, and test. Held-out citation reward rises by 0.079, from 0.702 to 0.781, with a bootstrap 95\% confidence interval of [0.051, 0.107], and the gain replicates across three seeds (mean 0.107). Nearly half of the winning prompt is a memorized answer key, yet a controlled ablation shows this content earns no reward even on the exact training questions it encodes. The large validation-minus-held-out gap is therefore small-sample selection under a noisy judge, not memorization. Varying the verifier isolates what makes the gain transfer. Decomposed per-dimension feedback is the causal ingredient, and collapsing it to a single scalar reward makes held-out transfer negative. The gain persists under an out-of-family judge and is largely reproduced by a hand-written version of the discovered recipe. Credible evaluation of prompt optimizers therefore requires held-out topics, multiple seeds, and verifier ablations. We release the candidate prompts, lineage diffs, figures, tables, and analysis.

</details>


### [134] [Self-Preservation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12602)
*Dongkyu Cho,Rumi Chunara,Ali Behrouz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究持续指令微调中"自我保存"问题：用冻结模型生成回放数据、甚至选择回放目标，能否更广泛地保护模型原有能力。基于Qwen多规格模型的实验揭示三项发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习旨在适配预训练模型的同时保留旧能力，但用于"保留"的任务只覆盖模型所知的一部分，遗忘可能超出这些任务之外。近期研究表明语言模型可在无原始训练数据的情况下生成有用的回放数据，由此引出"自我保存"问题：模型能否帮助选择更广泛保护自身能力的保留目标。

Method: 将任务描述及其样例组织为"任务库"（Task Banks），其条目可被选取与组合，用于引导冻结模型生成回放数据；在不同规模的Qwen模型上进行持续指令微调实验，并跟踪此前答对的样本以检测被平均准确率掩盖的损失。

Result: 三点发现：①新任务与学习率决定哪些旧能力退化，跟踪此前正确回答可揭示平均准确率掩盖的损失；②生成回放可减少任务库之外任务的遗忘，组合部分任务库可增强该保护，在测试的各规模上均优于单一任务库；③模型还能帮助选择回放目标——在新任务信息指导下选择，4B规模上提升留出集平均保留率且新任务收益相近，但较小模型无此优势；在固定样例预算下，用回放替换新任务样例可提升保留但牺牲新任务收益。

Conclusion: 模型可通过生成回放数据、并在某些设置下选择回放任务，来支持更广泛的自我保存。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks to adapt pretrained models while preserving prior capabilities. Yet the tasks supplied for preservation cover only part of what a model knows, while forgetting can extend beyond them. Recent work shows that language models can supplement this limited evidence by generating useful replay without access to their original training data. This raises a question about self-preservation: can a model help choose preservation targets that protect its own capabilities more broadly? To study this question, we organize task descriptions and their examples into Task Banks, whose entries can be selected and combined to guide replay generation by a frozen model. Continual instruction tuning with Qwen models of varying sizes yields three findings. First, the new task and learning rate shape which prior capabilities degrade, while tracking previously correct answers reveals losses that average accuracy can conceal. Second, generated replay reduces forgetting on tasks outside the bank. Combining partial banks strengthens this protection, improving mean retention of previously correct answers over either bank alone across the tested sizes. Third, the model can also help choose replay targets: selection informed by the new task improves mean held-out retention over random selection at 4B, with similar new-task gains, although smaller models show no corresponding advantage. Under a fixed example budget, however, replacing new-task examples with replay improves preservation at the cost of smaller new-task gains. Together, these findings show that models can support broader self-preservation by generating replay data and, in some settings, choosing which tasks to replay.

</details>


### [135] [GeopoliticalQA: Benchmarking Retrieval and Generation over Intelligence Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12617)
*Veronika Solopova,Ivan Havlytskyi,Viktoria Skorik,Ostap Vykhopen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个面向地缘政治与战略情报报告的问答基准，并系统评测了RAG的检索与生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地缘政治与战略情报报告蕴含丰富专家知识且价值日益增长，但其内容异质性和分析复杂性给检索增强问答带来挑战，缺乏相应的评测基准。

Method: 构建包含415份开源情报报告、5525个文档依据问答对（覆盖事实、推理、比较、风险四类问题）的基准；提出先对报告画像再生成问题的文档感知生成流程；通过专家标注和LLM-as-judge协议验证数据质量；评测15种检索系统和6个开源权重LLM。

Result: 文档级检索上各模型表现相近；在更难的块级检索中，检索质量并非由嵌入模型规模决定——中等规模开源编码器bge-m3可与大一个数量级的模型匹敌，并优于商业嵌入API。生成方面即使给出黄金证据仍具挑战性，推理型和风险型问题尤为困难。

Conclusion: 嵌入模型规模并非块级检索质量的决定因素，中等开源模型即可达到优异效果；但情报领域RAG的答案生成仍是难点，尤其是推理与风险类问题。

Abstract: Geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports constitute a rich source of expert knowledge and have increasing value in the current political climate, but their heterogeneity and analytical complexity pose challenges for retrieval-augmented question answering. We introduce a benchmark for grounded question answering over 415 open-source geopolitical and strategic intelligence reports, comprising 5,525 document-grounded question-answer pairs spanning factual, inferential, comparative, and risk-oriented questions. We propose a document-aware generation pipeline that profiles reports before generating questions and validate dataset quality through expert annotation and an LLM-as-judge protocol. Using the benchmark, we evaluate 15 retrieval systems and six open-weight LLMs for RAG. We find that models perform similarly on document-level retrieval, while at the harder chunk level retrieval quality is not driven by embedding-model scale: bge-m3, a mid-size open encoder matches an order of magnitude larger ones and outperforms commercial embedding APIs. In contrast, answer generation remains challenging even given the gold evidence, particularly for inferential and risk-oriented questions.

</details>


### [136] [perturber: A Stateless Service for Semantics-Preserving Text Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12667)
*Khoi-Nguyen Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 perturber，一个无状态服务与 Python 库，每次调用仅施加一个语义保持扰动，来自涵盖 11 个家族、68 种扰动的官方目录。它基于单一原语，组合交由调用者链式调用，保证服务与库输出一致。评估聚焦工具本身（正确性、可复现性、成本），而非模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在干净基准上的评估高估了鲁棒性：真实输入带有扰动，而语义保持的变体常会改变模型答案。系统研究此类现象需要可复现、可移植地逐个施加扰动，但现有工具将扰动与基准测试栈耦合，未打包为可复用的变换层。

Method: 提出 perturber：无状态服务与 Python 库，每次调用仅施加一个语义保持扰动，扰动取自第一方目录（11 个家族、68 种扰动）。核心为单一原语，组合方式由调用者链式调用实现，从而服务与库输出在构造上完全一致。提供可内省的目录用于基准选择，以及一键可复现评估。

Result: 对工具本身进行了评估，包括正确性、可复现性与成本；对每个扰动测量语义保持程度，而非直接假定。

Conclusion: perturber 作为可复用的扰动变换层，解耦了扰动与基准栈，支持可复现的逐个扰动应用，并附演示用实时游乐场与一键复现评估流程。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluations on clean benchmarks overstate model robustness: real inputs arrive perturbed, and these meaning-preserving variants routinely move model answers. Studying this systematically requires applying perturbations one at a time, reproducibly, and identically wherever they run, yet existing tools couple them to a benchmarking stack rather than packaging them as a reusable transform layer. We present perturber, a stateless service and Python library that applies exactly one semantics-preserving perturbation per call, drawn from a first-party catalog of 68 perturbations across 11 families. It turns on a single primitive, with composition left to the caller chaining calls, so the service and the library produce identical output by construction. We evaluate the tool itself, its correctness, reproducibility, and cost, rather than any model's robustness, measuring meaning-preservation per perturbation rather than assuming it. The demonstration adds a live playground, an introspectable catalog for benchmark selection, and a one-command reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [137] [GeoFitBench-LLM: A Benchmark for Assessing Large Language Models' Capabilities in Geospatial Data Requirements Elicitation for Geo-Analytic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12669)
*Amirhossein Vahdat,Thierry Badard,Luc Lamontagne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 GeoFitBench-LLM 基准，用于评估大语言模型能否从自然语言地理分析请求中推断所需数据集、支持资源与约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正成为 GeoAI 系统的核心，但其评估很少涉及时空数据需求的识别与推导。若无法先确定必要数据集与约束条件，面向检索的地理分析任务就难以可靠支持，这一缺失十分关键。

Method: 构建 GeoFitBench-LLM 基准，围绕两个推理维度设计：时空关系推理与语义落地。真值需求被编码为固定且与数据提供方无关的模式，以支持资源识别与属性级规范的可复现评估。对七个模型在最小提示条件下进行实验。

Result: 实验显示，识别必要资源通常可以实现，GPT-5.4 Thinking 表现最佳，在 66.67% 的基准请求中恢复出完整资源集合；但随着推理需求增加，准确的适用性规范仍然更为困难。

Conclusion: GeoFitBench-LLM 为评估大语言模型在地理分析数据需求推导方面的能力提供了基准，结果表明确认资源相对可行，而适用性规范在高推理复杂度下仍具挑战。

Abstract: Large language models are becoming central to GeoAI systems, yet their evaluation rarely considers the elicitation of geospatial data requirements. This omission is critical as retrieval oriented geo-analytic tasks cannot be supported reliably without first identifying the necessary datasets and constraints.
